A 54ms per fixture, 600 fixture farebbero 32 secondi, dentro il budget. **DUE MODI IN CUI IL TEST ESAGERA LA REALTÀ** (uno corretto, uno annotato): la fase giornata martellava a 500ms fissi per tutta la durata del tick — mille client che aggiornano senza pause per sedici minuti sono un assedio, non un picco — e ora usa il think time del giro quotidiano; e i 125 gironi sono seminati in un minuto quindi i loro calci d'inizio coincidono al secondo, mentre una ladder vera vede i gironi partire quando si riempiono e le giornate sparpagliarsi nell'arco del giorno. **PROSSIMO PASSO deciso con l'utente:** nuovo **`--scenario tick`** che semina la coorte e cronometra la STESSA chiamata a macchina scarica, stampando il costo per fixture — così si separa "il job è lento" da "la macchina è piena" — e solo dopo si decide se ottimizzare ancora (candidato in canna: `ReplayJson` salva il MatchReport COMPLETO, position stream incluso, ~180KB per partita e ~108MB per ogni giornata di ladder, mentre la doc di `PositionStream` dice che lo stream è derivato deterministicamente da seed + formazioni e "non ha mai bisogno di viaggiare in rete" → il client potrebbe rigenerarlo). **DA FARE SU DATABASE PULITO** (`docker compose down -v; docker compose up -d --build`): il fast-forward risolve OGNI stagione in corso, quindi una coorte avanzata gonfia il lavoro. Comando: `.\tools\loadtest.ps1 -Users 1000 -Scenario tick`.
- **9.6 RUN 1 FATTO dall'utente (due giri) + TRE CORREZIONI SCRITTE, in attesa di rebuild e ri-misura. A 200 coach: 11/11 check verdi (baseline 768 req/s p95 50ms, asta 396 req/s p95 8ms con 1839 offerte accettate e ZERO perse, job giornata 160 fixture in 7,0s). A 1000 coach: 8/11 (baseline 1490 req/s p50 408ms p95 872ms con ZERO 5xx e zero connessioni cadute; asta 1958 req/s p95 19ms, 9105 offerte accettate, ZERO perse e zero mismatch — la semplificazione senza `RowVersion` documentata da 8.5/9.2b ha retto sotto contesa vera; job giornata 660 fixture in 766s contro un budget di 60s).** **TRE SCOPERTE E LE RISPETTIVE CORREZIONI:** (1) **il tick della giornata non scalava** — 44ms per fixture con 25 gironi, 1160ms con 125: 26× peggio per unità di lavoro, quindi superlineare, quindi non "il portatile è lento". `ResolveDueMatchdaysAsync` girava su tutti i gironi con UN solo `DbContext` senza mai svuotare il change tracker: dopo 125 gironi si portava dietro ~22.000 giocatori tracciati più il `ReplayJson` di ogni partita già risolta, e ogni `SaveChanges` successivo pagava per scandirli. Ora ogni girone è una sua unità di lavoro (`BeginGroupAsync` svuota il tracker e ricarica il girone), stesso trattamento per avvio stagioni, finestre di mercato e reset. (2) **niente guardia anti-sovrapposizione** — il job è schedulato ogni minuto e un run ne durava dodici: ora `RankedSeasonJob.ExecuteAsync` ha `[DisableConcurrentExecution(3600)]` (lock distribuito, vale anche fra istanze) e `TickAsync` ha un gate di processo, perché gli endpoint dev (`/internal/ranked/tick`, fast-forward) sono un'altra strada verso lo stesso motore e durante il run ticchettavano dentro il job — ed è anche il motivo per cui il seed ha riportato solo 20 stagioni avviate a fronte di 112 finestre aperte. `FastForwardAsync` tiene il gate per tutto il ciclo, così spostare l'orologio e ticchettare è una mossa atomica. Nuova opzione FACOLTATIVA `Ranked:MaxMatchdaysPerTick` (0 = spenta, comportamento di sempre) per limitare un singolo run; quando è attiva i gironi sono serviti PIÙ IN RITARDO PRIMA e poi per chi ha atteso di più, così il tetto non può affamare la coda della ladder. (3) **un difetto dello strumento** — i 124.511 "4xx" della fase giornata erano 401: gli access token durano 15 minuti e quella fase ne è durata 13, quindi la coda del run misurava richieste non autenticate, che non toccano il database e quindi abbellivano il p95. Ora la sessione si rinnova col refresh token prima della scadenza (il seeding restituisce entrambi), il 401 ha un suo bucket e uno solo fa fallire il run. I 20.283 4xx dell'asta invece sono sani: sono `bid_too_low`, otto allenatori che si contendono davvero lo stesso lotto. **DECISIONE sul p95** (con l'utente): la latenza a 1000 vale come baseline RELATIVA — generatore, API, Postgres e Redis si dividevano i core dello stesso portatile, il p50 è passato da 15ms a 408ms mentre il throughput raddoppiava, cioè una macchina al soffitto e non qualcosa di rotto — e i 300ms restano il criterio per staging col generatore su un'altra macchina. Da guardare in quel giro: `POST /ranked/today/confirm` è l'endpoint più caro (p50 857ms a 1000). **NUOVI TEST** `Api.Tests/RankedTickScaleTests` (+ `RankedCappedTickTests`): un tick risolve la giornata di OGNI girone e persiste ogni mondo (l'hash di stato di ogni coorte si muove — è ciò che una scrittura persa romperebbe), tick concorrenti non risolvono due volte la stessa giornata (verifica aritmetica, non a tempo: le giornate riportate devono coincidere con le fixture salvate), e un tick col tetto serve i gironi a turno invece di affamarli. Attesi ~160 verdi (157 + 3). **RI-MISURA:** `cd server && dotnet test Api.Tests/Api.Tests.csproj`, poi `docker compose down -v` e `docker compose up -d --build` (l'ottimizzazione è codice server: il container del primo run NON ce l'ha, e un DB pulito evita che le coorti vecchie falsino i numeri), poi `.\tools\loadtest.ps1` e `.\tools\loadtest.ps1 -Users 1000 -Seconds 120`. File server toccati in questo giro: RankedSeasonService, RankedSeasonJob, RankedOptions, DependencyInjection, appsettings, DevSeedContracts/DevSeedService (refresh token nel seed) + il nuovo RankedTickScaleTests; lato tool: CoachSession.cs (nuovo), LoadClient/Metrics/Scenarios/Program.
- **9.6 Load test (1000 utenti simultanei, picco di giornata, picco d'asta) — SCRITTO, in attesa del run dell'utente. NESSUNA modifica a Sim.Core, NESSUNO schema nuovo → 208 Sim.Core + golden master 0xCDEA5A2F7B9E5CF6 intatti, niente migrazione, niente save bump; lato server c'è SOLO dev tooling (un endpoint di seeding dietro il gate `Dev:ExposeSeedEndpoints` già esistente) quindi `dotnet test Api.Tests` deve restare 157 verde.** Decisioni con l'utente (4): (1) generatore = console .NET nel repo (`tools/LoadTest`), niente k6/PowerShell — nessuna toolchain nuova, percentili calcolati in casa, e PS 5.1 non regge onestamente mille socket concorrenti; (2) cohort seminata IN-PROCESS via endpoint dev — mille register+enrol via HTTP costano minuti e mescolerebbero il setup con la misura; (3) tutto parametrico con default ridotto (200), `-Users 1000` è il run del requisito; (4) si gira su docker compose locale e i numeri valgono come baseline RELATIVA, con la nota esplicita che un portatile che divide i core fra generatore, API, Postgres e Redis non è un server → il p95 assoluto va riconfermato in staging. **SERVER (solo dev tooling):** `DevSeedContracts` += `DevLoadSeedRequest(Coaches,Ticks)`/`DevLoadAccountDto`/`DevLoadSeedResult`; `IDevSeedService.SeedLoadCohortAsync`; `DevSeedService` (il ctor prende ora anche `IRankedSeasonService` — risolto da DI, nessuno lo costruisce a mano → i 5 DevSeedTests restano verdi) crea N account freschi `loadbot{batch}x{i}@dev.local`, li iscrive col VERO `IRankedService.EnrolAsync` (i gironi di piazzamento si riempiono e i mondi nuovi si aprono come in una corsa del giorno di lancio) e poi tira i tick richiesti così le stagioni sono in corso con la finestra di mercato 0 aperta; restituisce ogni account CON il suo access token, perché l'hashing password di Identity è volutamente costoso e mille login dominerebbero la rampa. `ChangeTracker.Clear()` fra un coach e l'altro: una sola richiesta materializza un mondo generato per ogni girone pieno e altrimenti EF finirebbe a tracciare decine di migliaia di entità. `DevEndpoints` += `POST /internal/dev/ranked/load-seed?coaches=&ticks=` (query param così un POST senza body lega — la lezione di `/ranked/fill`). NOTA: le euristiche multi-account NON sparpagliano la cohort — `LinkedUserIdsAsync` torna vuoto per un account senza signal e il seeding gira in-process senza impronta HTTP. **GENERATORE** `tools/LoadTest` (net10.0, solo `HttpClient` + `System.Text.Json`, volutamente FUORI dalla .sln così `dotnet test` non lo costruisce mai): `LoadOptions` (url/users/coaches/seed-ticks/seconds/auction-seconds/think/p95/matchday-budget/scenario), `LoadClient` (un client condiviso col cap di connessioni per server alzato alla concorrenza, timeout per richiesta, e una richiesta annullata dal clock della fase NON registrata come errore), `Metrics` (coda lock-free, p50/p90/p95/p99 complessivi e per endpoint; un 429 è "rate-limited" e un 4xx è "rifiutato" — nessuno dei due è un fallimento, sono risposte), `BidLedger`, `Scenarios`. **Tre forme:** *baseline* = il vero giro quotidiano (`/ranked/today`, `/ranked/season`, `/ranked/offers`, e ogni terzo giro `POST /ranked/today/confirm`) con think time, perché mille utenti simultanei non sono una carica; *asta* = si offre a `minNextBid` sui lotti condivisi dal girone, quindi la contesa è vera (otto allenatori sullo stesso lotto) invece che un account che martella (che il limiter 9.5 taglierebbe comunque); *giornata* = un `POST /internal/ranked/fast-forward?matchdays=1` risolve la giornata di tutta la ladder mentre tutti aggiornano, e la sua durata È la scadenza che conta, visto che il job ricorrente gira ogni minuto. **Il check "nessuna offerta persa"** è quello interessante: ogni offerta ACCETTATA viene registrata con l'importo, poi ogni allenatore rilegge i suoi lotti e si verifica che l'offerta più alta finale non sia mai sotto il massimo accettato. Un lotto ranked porta il leader sulla riga SENZA token di concorrenza ottimistica (semplificazione v1 documentata da 8.5/9.2b) → se questo check fallisce ha trovato un bug vero (e giustifica una `RowVersion`), non è un test ballerino. **Check + exit code** (PASS/FAIL per riga come gli smoke): tutti iscritti, p95 nel budget per fase, l'asta aveva lotti, zero offerte perse, zero mismatch scrittura/lettura, zero 5xx, zero connessioni cadute, job giornata nella finestra e con fixture davvero risolte. `tools/loadtest.ps1` (ASCII-only, PS 5.1-safe, `-BaseUrl -Users -Coaches -Seconds -AuctionSeconds -Scenario -P95 -MatchdayBudget`) compila, lancia e propaga l'exit code. **HANDOFF:** niente `shared/` → niente build-simcore; `cd server && dotnet test Api.Tests/Api.Tests.csproj` (attesi 157 verdi), poi `docker compose up -d --build`, poi `.\tools\loadtest.ps1` (regressione rapida a 200) e `.\tools\loadtest.ps1 -Users 1000 -Seconds 120` per la ✅ del requisito. Il seed a 1000 richiede minuti (125 gironi di piazzamento, ognuno materializza il suo mondo) e lascia molte righe: `docker compose down -v` fra un run completo e l'altro, anche perché dopo un giro l'intera cohort condivide un indirizzo in `account_signals`. Nuovi file server (nessuno — solo edit a DevSeedContracts/IDevSeedService/DevSeedService/DevEndpoints), nuovi file tool (LoadTest.csproj, LoadOptions.cs, LoadClient.cs, Metrics.cs, BidLedger.cs, Scenarios.cs, Program.cs, loadtest.ps1) — niente .meta Unity. Tenere 9.6 a [~] finché il run non è giudicato insieme.

- **9.5a BRING-UP (1° smoke Docker dell'utente): 7 check rossi, tutti a cascata da UNO — "unrelated newcomers still share a cohort" FAIL. Su una sola macchina ogni account nasce dallo stesso indirizzo a pochi secondi dagli altri = esattamente il pattern che le euristiche separano, quindi buyer e seller finivano in gironi diversi; da lì il club id del "seller" veniva risolto DENTRO il girone del buyer (gli external id dei club sono per-mondo 1..8) e cadeva spesso sul club del buyer stesso → 400 "non puoi comprare un tuo giocatore" e 400 "non puoi segnalare te stesso", zero flag di trasferimento. Il server si comportava correttamente: era lo SCRIPT a essere mal disegnato per un host singolo. FIX (a) SERVER: `IntegritySignalMiddleware` ora registra il fingerprint PRIMA dell'endpoint, non dopo la risposta — un account il cui primissimo colpo ranked è `POST /ranked/enrol` non aveva ancora un'impronta e scavalcava la guardia (buco vero, chiuso). FIX (b) SMOKE: la controparte di scambio è ora un BOT dello stesso girone (`/internal/dev/ranked/fill` + l'autopilota `/internal/dev/ranked/{groupId}/market/bot?accept=true` per l'accettazione), cioè come lo testa davvero un giocatore solo; i prezzi sono calcolati sul budget reale (regalo = 1% del top player; sovrapprezzo = 3× su un giocatore che ci sta nei 25M, altrimenti SKIP con nota invece di FAIL; scambio onesto = valore pieno del più economico affrontabile) e il report va al club del bot. VERIFICATO nel run: rate limiter 10 serviti / 30 rifiutati su 40 bid, deadline formazione prima del kickoff accettata, i due account sullo stesso device separati in gironi diversi + flag. NOTA economia emersa dal run: in un mondo ranked pieno il top player vale ~161M contro un budget di 25M — le offerte dirette toccano solo la fascia bassa/media della rosa; da tenere presente per il bilanciamento 10.1.**

Atteso al 2° run: 157/157 verdi.**

Override `Integrity__SignalSalt` in any real environment.** Decisions with the user up front (4): (1) rate limit = middleware .NET nativo in-memory, partizionato per ACCOUNT; (2) collusione = BLOCCO duro fuori banda + FLAG nella fascia grigia; (3) multi-account = flag + guardia in matchmaking (mai ban); (4) server + test prima, client 9.5b dopo. **Posizione di fondo:** rendere inutile il farming, non arbitrare il gioco — ogni guardia o rifiuta ciò che non può nascere da una trattativa vera, o annota qualcosa di strano perché un umano lo guardi; niente ban, nessun risultato cambiato di nascosto. **Modelli PURI** (`Application/Integrity`, no I/O, no RNG, testati da soli come i modelli di bilanciamento Sim.Core): `TransferIntegrity.Assess(fee, marketValue, bands)` → Fair/Suspicious/Blocked e `LinkHeuristics.Score(evidence, weights)` → 0..100 con la proprietà VOLUTA che **un indirizzo condiviso da solo NON supera la soglia** (casa, coinquilini, NAT dell'operatore sono indistinguibili) mentre un device condiviso sì. **Collusione** in `RankedMarketService`: fee fuori da `[MinFeePercentOfValue, MaxFeePercentOfValue]` (40%..250% del valore di mercato) → `RankedError.IntegrityBlocked` → 409 `integrity_blocked` + riga nella coda di revisione; **ri-verifica all'ACCEPT** (i valori si muovono con lo sviluppo, e l'accept è quando i soldi si muovono davvero); affare concluso in fascia grigia (<65% / >160%) → passa ma viene flaggato, come pure la STESSA COPPIA di allenatori che commercia 3 volte nello stesso girone. Budget e taglia minima rosa restano PRIMA della banda (un'offerta oltre budget resta 400). **Multi-account**: nuova tabella `account_signals` con hash SHA-256 salato di indirizzo + device id opzionale (`X-Fts-Device`) — **mai un IP in chiaro**, e ruotare `Integrity:SignalSalt` rende incorrelabile tutto lo storico; `RankedService.EnrolAsync` chiede gli account collegati e SALTA ogni girone in formazione che ne contiene uno; se tutti sono bloccati parte il normale "apri un nuovo girone di piazzamento", quindi la guardia non può MAI impedire l'iscrizione — decide solo in QUALE coorte finisci. **Deadline input**: `SubmitLineupAsync` rifiuta quando il calcio d'inizio della prossima giornata è passato (`RankedError.DeadlinePassed` → 409), chiudendo la finestra fra kickoff e job minutely in cui si poteva scegliere la formazione dopo aver visto gli altri risultati; rifiutare non costa nulla perché 9.4 garantisce che una formazione salvata c'è sempre. **Report**: `POST /ranked/report` contro un allenatore del TUO girone (per club external id — ciò che il client mostra), con tetto per finestra e per giorno e risposta volutamente scarna (non si può usare per sondare altri account). **Rate limit**: `AddRateLimiter` nativo, tre bucket per-account — bid 10/10s, scritture ranked 30/10s, report 3/10s — applicati SOLO ai POST, così il polling GET del client non è toccato; il rifiuto è un 429 nello stesso involucro d'errore del resto della API. **Coda di revisione** `integrity_flags`: append-only e SENZA FK (come `ranked_awards`) così la traccia sopravvive a gruppo/mondo/reset; `GET /internal/ranked/integrity/flags` la legge, dev-gated, finché la dashboard admin 10.3 non la prende in carico. **Api.Tests** (~12 nuovi): `TransferIntegrityTests` + `LinkHeuristicsTests` puri, poi integrazione — **THE ✅** `LopsidedTransfers_AreRefused_AndFlaggedForReview` (regalo E sovrapprezzo entrambi rifiutati, entrambi in coda, il giocatore non si muove), `AGreyBandTransfer_GoesThrough_ButIsFlagged`, **THE ✅** `BidSpam_IsRateLimited` (limiti stretti a 3/min in quella fixture: deterministico invece di correre contro una finestra reale), **THE ✅** `LinkedAccounts_JoinTheLadder_ButNotTheSameGroup` (+ un nuovo arrivato NON collegato riempie comunque il girone più vecchio → il matchmaking non è disturbato), deadline rifiutata dopo il kickoff E accettata prima (fixture con intervallo reale, così la guardia non può "funzionare" rifiutando tutto), report filed/self-report 400. **NOTA sui test:** la base condivisa ora DISABILITA le euristiche multi-account (ogni account di una fixture nasce dallo stesso host a pochi secondi dagli altri = esattamente il pattern che le euristiche notano; lasciandole accese ogni coorte si sparpaglia e nessuna stagione parte), e la fixture che le testa le riaccende. Cinque `RankedMarketTests` esistenti aggiornati per scambiare a prezzo ONESTO (offrivano 1M fissi per il miglior giocatore del club = esattamente il "campione regalato" che ora si blocca). **DEV-SIM TOOLING** (regola permanente): `tools/smoke-ranked-abuse.ps1` (ASCII-only, PS 5.1-safe, account freschi ogni run, PASS/FAIL per scenario, exit 0/1): due account sullo stesso device finiscono in gironi diversi, regalo e sovrapprezzo rifiutati mentre lo scambio onesto passa, lo spam di offerte fa scattare il limiter, la formazione prima del kickoff è accettata, i report funzionano, e la coda flag è stampata per tipo. Decisions with the user up front (4): (1) la home ranked DIVENTA il digest (una schermata sola per il loop quotidiano); (2) aggregazione lato SERVER con un nuovo `GET /ranked/today` + test (riusabile per notifiche/artifact); (3) TUTTI e quattro gli smart default (formazione auto-persistita e riparata + allenamento con default salvato + "Conferma giornata" in un tap + badge cose-da-fare); (4) server + test prima, client (9.4b) dopo.** **Application** `Ranked/RankedTodayContracts` (`RankedTodoKind` {Enrol=0/ConfirmMatchday=1/RespondOffer=2/MarketWindow=3/SeasonSummary=4} + `RankedTodoDto(Kind,Count,Priority)` — NESSUN testo utente sul filo, il client localizza per kind, come ogni altro DTO ranked; `RankedTodayNextMatchDto`/`RankedTodayLastResultDto`; `RankedTodayDto` = stato+rating+girone+club, calendario [TotalRounds/RoundsPlayed/NextRound/SeasonComplete], la tua posizione+punti, prossima partita (con `SecondsToKickoff`) e ultimo risultato dal TUO punto di vista, i tuoi input [LineupReady/LineupConfirmed/TrainingSet/TrainingTeamFocus], mercato [finestra + budget + offerte in/out + lotti aperti + lotti in cui sei in testa], `ActionCount` + `Todo` ordinata per priorità; `NotEnrolled()` = digest valido per un account mai iscritto, quindi l'endpoint non fa MAI 404 a un utente loggato) + `IRankedTodayService`. **Infrastructure** `Ranked/RankedTodayService` (PROIEZIONE pura sullo stato che la ladder già possiede: seat→gruppo, fixtures, `RankedStandings.Compute` [stessa tabella che premia il season end], `RankedCalendar.CurrentWindow`, conteggi offerte/aste; l'UNICA scrittura è `ConfirmMatchdayAsync`) + `Ranked/RankedInputDefaults` (gli SMART DEFAULT: `SeedForGroupAsync` alla partenza stagione salva per ogni seat umano una `LineupPlan.From(LineupSelector.BestEleven)` + un `TrainingPlan.Balanced()`; `EnsureLineupAsync`/`RepairAfterSquadChangeAsync` ricostruiscono la formazione quando un titolare viene venduto — tattica e piano pre-partita CONSERVATI; `EnsureTrainingAsync`; + `RankedPlanJson` = le opzioni JSON condivise, ora usate anche da `RankedSeasonService.PlanJson`). L'XI seminata è ESATTAMENTE quella che il motore avrebbe scelto come fallback silenzioso → nessun risultato cambia, ma il digest può dire il vero su "formazione pronta". **Nuova entità** `RankedTraining` (`ranked_trainings`, gemella ranked di `LeagueTraining` 8.4: cascade dal gruppo, club FK Restrict, unique (group,club)) + `RankedLineup.ConfirmedRound` (int, 0 = mai confermata; puramente informativo — il calendario risolve comunque la formazione salvata, dimenticare di confermare non fa MAI perdere a tavolino). **Wiring:** `RankedSeasonService.GenerateScheduleAsync` semina i default; `ResolveRoundAsync` ora passa i piani salvati a `OnlineSeasonTick.EvolveWeek` via `LoadTrainingPlansAsync` (era `NoTraining` per tutti → i club senza riga restano sul default AI, esattamente come prima); `SubmitLineupAsync` timbra `ConfirmedRound` (chi sceglie la formazione non viene più stuzzicato dal digest); nuovi `SubmitTrainingAsync`/`GetMyTrainingAsync` su `IRankedSeasonService` + `SubmitRankedTrainingRequest`; `RankedMarketService.RespondAsync` (accept) ripara la formazione del VENDITORE; `RankedService.MoveToTierAsync` semina lineup+training quando un promosso/retrocesso atterra su un girone la cui stagione è già in corso (il quirk v1 dei calendari indipendenti); `RankedSeasonEndService.RebuildGroupAsync` cancella anche `ranked_trainings` al reset. **Api** += `GET /ranked/today`, `POST /ranked/today/confirm` (idempotente: semina/ripara gli input e timbra il prossimo turno), `POST /ranked/training`, `GET /ranked/training` (JSON del piano o `{}`); DI registra `IRankedTodayService`. NESSUNA nuova chiave di config. **Api.Tests `RankedTodayTests`** (9, sulla base `RankedSeasonTestBase` = piramide ridotta + calendario compresso, un tick avvia la stagione e risolve la 1ª giornata): **THE ✅** `Digest_InSeason_ShowsTheWholeDay_AndOneTapConfirmClearsTheChore` (posizione/ultimo risultato/prossima partita/formazione pronta ma non confermata → confirm → confermata, la voce sparisce dalla lista, secondo confirm no-op), **THE ✅** `SmartDefault_RepairsTheStoredLineup_WhenAStarterIsSold` (vendo un titolare della XI salvata → la formazione resta di 11 giocatori distinti senza il venduto), digest per non iscritto (solo "Enrol") / iscritto senza stagione (0 azioni), offerte in arrivo = voce più urgente, default seminati (11 slot distinti + focus Balanced), allenamento inviato che sopravvive a un tick, guardie confirm 404/409 e 401 senza token. **HANDOFF:** NO shared/ change → NIENTE build-simcore (208 Sim.Core + golden master restano); `cd server && dotnet ef migrations add AddRankedDailyLoop -p Infrastructure -s Api` (additiva: SOLO `ranked_trainings` + la colonna `ConfirmedRound`) + commit `Migrations/*`, poi `dotnet test Api.Tests/Api.Tests.csproj` (~141 verdi, `[server-determinism] 0xCDEA5A2F7B9E5CF6` invariato), poi `docker compose up -d --build` + smoke: iscriviti, `POST /internal/dev/ranked/fill`, `POST /internal/ranked/tick` → `GET /ranked/today` mostra prossima partita/ultimo risultato/posizione/finestra + `todo` con `confirmMatchday` → `POST /ranked/today/confirm` → la voce sparisce; `POST /ranked/training {"training":{"teamFocus":"Attacking"}}` → `GET /ranked/training`. Nuovi .cs server (RankedTodayContracts, RankedTodayService, RankedInputDefaults, RankedTraining, RankedTodayTests) + edit (RankedLineup, FtsDbContext, RankedSeasonContracts, RankedSeasonService, RankedSeasonEndService, RankedMarketService, RankedService, RankedEndpoints, DependencyInjection) — niente .meta Unity. Limite documentato: nessuna voce "sei stato superato all'asta" (un lotto ranked memorizza solo il leader corrente, senza storico offerte in v1 → il digest riporta i lotti in cui sei IN TESTA, che è ciò che i dati sanno davvero); e un coach Retired (uscito dalla ladder) vede "iscritto, nessuna azione" perché ri-iscriversi non è ancora una capability 9.1. Tenere 9.4 a [~] finché migrazione/test/up non passano; poi 9.4b = client Unity (la home ranked diventa il digest + pulsante Conferma giornata + schermata allenamento ranked).

NEW `Views/RankedLeaderboardView` (dumb: header + summary + due tab [Classifica / Palmarès] + Aggiorna, lista di card `RowVm` [Title/Detail/Highlight=riga tua in Accent/Trophy=titolo o promozione in Positive], status + Back; solo primitive, nessun ref Sim.Core/Services — verificato) + `Presenters/RankedLeaderboardScreenPresenter` (App scope, `Push<T>`-resolved: deps ScreenNavigator/ILocalizationService/RankedApiService già App singleton; tab 0 = classifica top-50 con la RIGA TUA appesa in fondo quando sei fuori dalla fetta [il server la restituisce in `you`], tab 1 = palmarès con riepilogo [rating/record/stagioni/titoli/promozioni/retrocessioni] + una riga per premio; carica una volta per tab e tiene in cache fino ad Aggiorna — niente poll, questi dati non cambiano di secondo in secondo). `RankedHomeView`/`Presenter` += pulsante "Classifica e palmarès" (visibile solo da iscritto) → `Push<RankedLeaderboardScreenPresenter>`. `RankedSeasonView` += una CARD di fine stagione sopra la tabella (`SetSeasonEnd(title, detail, visible, celebrate, setback)` — titolo verde per titolo/promozione, rosso per retrocessione) e il commento del pulsante dev aggiornato; `RankedSeasonScreenPresenter` += `LoadSeasonEndAsync` (quando `seasonComplete`, legge il palmarès UNA volta per stagione conclusa — guardia `_seasonEndGroup` così il poll da 3s non rifà la fetch — trova il numero di stagione più alto per quel girone e ne compone posizione/rating/delta + eventuale titolo/promozione/retrocessione) e `OnAdvanceDev` che ora fa fill + `FastForwardDevAsync(3)` e azzera la guardia (il reset può essere avvenuto nel frattempo). (2) Elo per matchday + a finishing bonus at season end; (3) rose resettate del tutto tra stagioni (riequalizzate + budget uguali + nuova asta); (4) palmarès persistente (niente cosmetici per ora). **Pure `EloModel`** (expected 1/(1+10^((Rb−Ra)/400)), K·(actual−expected) rounded away from zero, linear finishing bonus +swing→−swing, promotion/relegation delta, rating floor) — server maths, NOT Sim.Core, so floating point is fine. **`RankedAward`** (`ranked_awards`, append-only, FK-FREE with world/group names captured by value so a palmarès outlives world/group/reset); `RankedCoach` += PeakRating/SeasonsPlayed; `RankedGroup` += SeasonEndedUtc/SeasonNumber. **`IRankedRankingService`/`RankedRankingService`** = the ONLY writer of `Rating` (ApplyMatchAsync / ApplySeasonEndAsync / GrantAwardAsync / RatingOfAsync) + reads GetLeaderboardAsync (Redis top-N ids hydrated from Postgres, SQL fallback + cache warm) and GetPalmaresAsync; `IRankedLeaderboardCache` → `RedisRankedLeaderboardCache` (sorted set `fts:ranked:leaderboard:v1`, swallows transport errors) or `NoOpRankedLeaderboardCache` (Testing / no Redis). **In-season Elo** in `ResolveRoundAsync`: both coaches rated per fixture, a vacant AI seat plays at `AiRatingForTier` (AiRatingTopTier − step per tier), placement groups skipped, both ratings read before either is written. **Season end in TWO steps with a break** (`IRankedSeasonEndService`): (1) `CompleteDivisionSeasonAsync` at the last matchday = finishing bonus + palmarès rows (TopFlightTitle/Champion, Promotion/Relegation, always SeasonPlayed) + group→Completed with `SeasonEndedUtc` so the FINAL TABLE STAYS READABLE; (2) `ApplySeasonResetAsync` after `SeasonBreakSeconds` (604800) = P/R via the new `IRankedService.MoveToTierAsync` (frees the old seat → AI, claims one in the target tier, materialising it; out-of-range/full = stay put so it can never stall), release of `AutoEnrol=false` coaches (Retired, rating+palmarès kept), wipe of fixtures/lineups/offers/auctions, `SquadEqualizer` re-equalisation + neutral condition + equal budgets, surplus beyond `SeasonResetSquadSize` (22) RELEASED TO FREE AGENCY (fixed squad sizes + a refilled auction pool, never empties a role), group reopened with SeasonNumber+1 → the 9.2 tick starts season 2 and its first window re-auctions the free agents. Seats are never created/destroyed → the 9.1 fixed-size invariant holds. `TickAsync` += `ApplyDueSeasonResetsAsync` (+ `RankedTickSummary.SeasonsReset`) and a per-season `SeasonSeed` (world seed splitmixed with SeasonNumber → schedule + match seeds + evolve week) so season 2 is not a replay; standings extracted to the shared pure `RankedStandings` (the table shown == the table rewarded). **DEV-SIM TOOLING:** `FastForwardAsync` + dev-gated `POST /internal/ranked/fast-forward?matchdays=N` — pulls every running season's start/kickoffs/auction ends AND any between-seasons break back one interval per step then ticks (real time travel, not a bypass) so a solo tester runs a season + reset + season 2 in seconds. **Api** += `GET /ranked/leaderboard?top=` / `GET /ranked/palmares` (JWT). **DI** registers ranking + season-end services and picks Redis-vs-NoOp cache from the same flag that gates background jobs; appsettings Ranked += EloKFactor 24 / AiRatingTopTier 1150 / AiRatingPerTierStep 100 / SeasonEndPositionSwing 40 / PromotionRatingBonus 60 / PromotionSlots 1 / RelegationSlots 1 / MinRating 100 / SeasonBreakSeconds 604800 / ResetSquadsBetweenSeasons true / SeasonResetSquadSize 22 / LeaderboardTopCount 50. **Api.Tests `RankedRankingTests`**: pure `EloModelTests` (6, property-based) + integration on a shrunk pyramid where the WHOLE cohort is placed into tier 2 → an ALL-HUMAN division, which makes champion/bottom (and therefore P/R) deterministic: **THE ✅** `TwoSeasons_RatingsFollowResults_PromotionAndRelegationApply_AndSeason2IsFairAgain`, leaderboard ordering/own-row, palmarès guards, opt-out release, + `RankedFastForwardTests` (real 1h spacing: plain tick = 1 matchday, fast-forward pulls several). This is the "comprare da altri giocatori mandando offerte" half of the market decision; the free-agent AUCTIONS (reuse 8.5) are the next increment.** Each ranked club is seeded a flat, equal `TransferBudget` (RankedOptions.StartingTransferBudget, default 25M) when its season starts (in `RankedSeasonService.GenerateScheduleAsync` — reuses the existing `Club.TransferBudget` column from Phase 8, so NO schema change for budgets). NEW entity `RankedOffer` (buyer user+club, seller user+club, player id/externalId, fee, WindowIndex, Status enum Pending/Accepted/Rejected/Withdrawn, timestamps; plain denormalised ids, single cascade path ranked_offers→ranked_groups) + `FtsDbContext` DbSet/config (cascade from ranked_groups; indexes on (group,status)/seller/buyer). `Application/Ranked/RankedMarketContracts` (RankedOfferStatus, RankedPlayerDto/RankedSquadDto/RankedOfferDto/RankedOffersDto/MakeRankedOfferRequest + `IRankedMarketService`); `RankedError` += `InsufficientBudget` (400). `Infrastructure/Ranked/RankedMarketService` — GetClubSquad (browse any club in your group), GetOffers (budget + incoming/outgoing + MarketOpen), MakeOffer (upsert one pending offer per (buyer,player); gated to an open window via `RankedCalendar.CurrentWindow`; guards NotEnrolled/WrongPhase-market-closed/ValidationFailed-free-agent/own-player/AI-club/InsufficientBudget), Respond accept|reject (seller only; accept re-validates budget + a `MinSquadSizeForSale` floor, moves `Player.ClubId` + `ContractSeasonsRemaining=4`, debits buyer/credits seller `TransferBudget`, logs a `Transfer` row — mirrors the 8.5 auction settlement; requires an open window), Withdraw (buyer, while Pending); best-effort `INotificationService` on every event. Budgets re-validated at accept time (no reservation — a documented v1 simplification like 8.5). DI registers `IRankedMarketService`; `RankedEndpoints` += `GET /ranked/clubs/{ext}/squad`, `GET /ranked/offers`, `POST /ranked/offers`, `POST /ranked/offers/{id}/accept|reject|withdraw` (JWT); MapError += insufficient_budget 400; appsettings Ranked += StartingTransferBudget/MinSquadSizeForSale (+ config binding in AddRanked). **Api.Tests**: `RankedMarketTests` (a full placement cohort fills ONE group with all-human seats → any two trade there): budgets seeded 25M at season start, browse a rival squad, **the ✅** make offer → owner accepts → player changes clubs + buyer budget −fee / seller +fee, over-budget 400, own-player 400, only-owner-can-accept 403 (buyer + bystander), buyer-withdraw; `RankedMarketClosedTests` (WindowSeconds→0 override on the shared base) → make offer refused 409. HANDOFF: NO shared/ change → NO build-simcore; `cd server && dotnet ef migrations add AddRankedOffers -p Infrastructure -s Api` (additive — ONLY the ranked_offers table; budgets ride the existing Club.TransferBudget column) + commit `Migrations/*`, then `dotnet test Api.Tests` (expect ~116 green: 109 prior + 7 new), then `docker compose up` + a live smoke: enrol a cohort, one `POST /internal/ranked/tick` (season starts, window opens, budgets 25M), `GET /ranked/clubs/{ext}/squad` → `POST /ranked/offers {playerExternalId, fee}` → the owner `POST /ranked/offers/{id}/accept` moves the player. NEXT: free-agent auctions in the windows (reuse/generalise 8.5), then the Unity ranked client.

SERVER-ONLY, NO Sim.Core change → 208 Sim.Core + golden master 0xCDEA5A2F7B9E5CF6 UNTOUCHED. This completes the market decision (auctions + the direct offers of increment 2). 🏁 9.2b SERVER is complete (condition/dev evolution + direct offers + free-agent auctions all green); the remaining 9.2 piece is the Unity ranked CLIENT.** Ranked worlds already carry free agents (WorldFactory.Build seeds them via FreeAgentFactory, 8.5). NEW entity `RankedAuction` (free-agent lot per group: PlayerId, WindowIndex, StartPrice, HighBid + HighBidClub/User denormalised, Status Open/Settled/Unsold, EndsUtc=window close; single cascade path ranked_auctions→ranked_groups; the current leader lives on the lot, NO separate bid-history table in v1) + `FtsDbContext` DbSet/config. `Application/Ranked/RankedAuctionContracts` (RankedAuctionStatus, RankedAuctionLotDto/RankedAuctionsDto[budget/committed/available/windowOpen]/PlaceRankedBidRequest/RankedBidResultDto + `IRankedAuctionService`); `RankedError` += AuctionNotFound(404)/AuctionClosed(409)/BidTooLow(400). `Infrastructure/Ranked/RankedAuctionService` — adapted from the 8.5 AuctionService (StartPrice 50% of MarketValue floored 25k; min raise 5%/floored 25k; committed-budget guard = budget − my other leading lots so a club can't win more than it can pay; no self-outbid) but **settled by the ranked tick, not Hangfire/SignalR** (a day-long ranked window needs no live anti-snipe): `OpenWindowLotsAsync` (idempotent per group+window, a lot per free agent, EndsUtc=window ClosesUtc), `PlaceBidAsync` (window-open + lot-open + min-bid + committed-budget guards; outbid notification), `SettleDueAsync(force)` (settle Open lots whose EndsUtc≤now — or all when forced — winner gets Player.ClubId + ContractSeasons=4 + charged, Transfer logged [FromClubId null free agent], else Unsold), `GetAuctionsAsync`. `RankedSeasonService.TickAsync` now calls `_auctions.OpenWindowLotsAsync(...)` when a window opens (in AnnounceMarketWindows) + `_auctions.SettleDueAsync(false)` each tick (RankedSeasonService ctor gained IRankedAuctionService — no DI cycle). DI registers `IRankedAuctionService`; `RankedEndpoints` += `GET /ranked/auctions`, `POST /ranked/auctions/{id}/bid` (JWT) + dev-gated `POST /internal/ranked/auctions/settle` (force-settle, the testable/staging path like 8.5's close-window); MapError += auction_not_found/auction_closed/bid_too_low. **Api.Tests** `RankedAuctionTests` (compressed calendar + the dev force-settle): a window opening creates free-agent lots + budget picture; **the ✅** a bids the cheapest lot → b outbids at MinNextBid → force settle → b gets the player + is charged the winning bid, a (outbid) is NOT charged, the settled lot leaves the open list; bid-below-min 400 / over-budget 400 / unknown-lot 404. HANDOFF: NO shared/ change → NO build-simcore; `cd server && dotnet ef migrations add AddRankedAuctions -p Infrastructure -s Api` (additive — ONLY the ranked_auctions table) + commit `Migrations/*`, then `dotnet test Api.Tests` (expect ~121 green: 116 prior + 5 new), then `docker compose up` + a live smoke: enrol a cohort, `POST /internal/ranked/tick` (window opens → lots), `GET /ranked/auctions` → `POST /ranked/auctions/{id}/bid {amount}`, `POST /internal/ranked/auctions/settle` → the top bidder gets the player + is charged. - **9.1 World & pyramid management (Phase 9 START) — server VERIFIED + Docker LIVE-SMOKED, [~] pending only commit Migrations/*. Live smoke PASSED: enrol → placement seat (Mondo 1/Placement 1, 8 fixed seats, 7 AI), resolve → Placed into "Divisione 2A" tier 2 on a lazily-materialised division world, /ranked/me = status Placed/tier 2/pos 1. `dotnet ef migrations add AddRankedWorlds` built OK; `dotnet test Api.Tests` 99/99 green (92 prior + 7 new ranked; `[server-determinism] 0xCDEA5A2F7B9E5CF6` unchanged; the 40-account spillover ✅ passes). SERVER-ONLY, NO Sim.Core change → 208 Sim.Core + golden master 0xCDEA5A2F7B9E5CF6 UNTOUCHED (Api pulls Sim.Core only transitively for world generation).** Decisions with the user up front (3): (1) pyramid = **3 tiers of 8-club groups** (tier 1 = 1 group, tier 2 = 2, tier 3 = 4 ⇒ 56 seats, 48 placeable; tier 1 is promotion-only); (2) **placement season** — a newcomer plays a placement group and is sorted into a division by finish; (3) **overflow = a new world opened automatically** when the existing ones are full. NEW EF entities `ranked_worlds`/`ranked_groups`/`ranked_seats`/`ranked_coaches`: a **RankedWorld** is one self-contained pyramid (distinct from the generated `World` — each of its groups owns its OWN generated world, **materialised LAZILY** the first time the group gets an occupant so opening a world is cheap = rows only); a **RankedGroup** is a girone (Placement or Division@tier) whose `Capacity` **RankedSeat** rows are created up front and never added-to/deleted (claiming a seat only sets `UserId` → a division ALWAYS has exactly GroupSize clubs, vacant = AI; unique idx (RankedGroupId,SeatIndex)); a **RankedCoach** is one row/account ever (unique UserId) holding the persistent `Rating` + seat. Cascade paths single (ranked_worlds→groups→seats; coach→world), club/world FKs SetNull so a world teardown can't shrink a division. **Application** `Ranked/` (RankedContracts enums+DTOs+`RankedResult<T>` mirroring LeagueResult; IRankedService). **Infrastructure** `Ranked/`: `RankedOptions` (pyramid shape, hand-bound from the "Ranked" config section like JwtOptions — GroupSize 8 / Tier{1,2,3}Groups {1,2,4} / PlacementTopPositionsToUpperTier 2 / StartingRating 1000); `RankedService` — Enrol (idempotent → a placement seat; opens a fresh placement group only in a world whose PLACEABLE capacity [free tier-≥2 seats minus seats reserved by pending placement cohorts] can absorb a whole cohort, else opens a brand-new world = the **spillover ✅**), GetMine/GetGroup/SetAutoEnrol, **ResolvePlacement** (top 2 finishers → tier 2, rest → tier 3 [tier 1 promotion-only], seed rating by finish, free the placement seats; a supplied final order is validated else a deterministic squad-strength order is used so it can always close). Per-group world seed derived (splitmix) from the ranked world's root seed → whole pyramid reproducible from one number. **Api** `Ranked/RankedEndpoints` (`POST /ranked/enrol`, `GET /ranked/me`, `GET /ranked/groups/{id}`, `POST /ranked/auto-enrol` — JWT, account id from token; + dev-gated internal `POST /internal/ranked/placement/{groupId}/resolve` behind `Ranked:ExposeInternalEndpoints`, the 9.2 scheduler calls the service directly later). DI `AddRanked` + `app.MapRankedEndpoints()`; `appsettings.json` += a `Ranked` section. **Api.Tests** `RankedEndpointTests` (behaviour: 401, not-enrolled, idempotent enrol + fixed-size seat list, auto-enrol toggle, unknown-group 404, resolve-twice 409) + `RankedPlacementSpilloverTests` (**THE ✅**, own private-DB fixture, shrunk 4-club pyramid via `WithWebHostBuilder` config ⇒ 8 placeable seats/world: 40 accounts → spill across 5 worlds / 10 placement groups, resolving all 10 sorts every one into tier-2/3 divisions that stay at exactly GroupSize seats). Tests use `EnsureCreated` (no migration needed). **HANDOFF:** NO shared/ change → NO build-simcore (208 + golden master stand); `cd server && dotnet ef migrations add AddRankedWorlds -p Infrastructure -s Api` (additive on `AddLiveMatch` — only the 4 ranked tables) + commit `Migrations/*`, then `dotnet test Api.Tests/Api.Tests.csproj` (expect ~97 green: 92 prior + ~5 new), then `docker compose up -d --build` + a bearer token: `POST /ranked/enrol` → a placement seat; `GET /ranked/me`; `POST /internal/ranked/placement/{groupId}/resolve` → sorted into a division. New server .cs (RankedContracts, IRankedService, RankedWorld, RankedGroup, RankedSeat, RankedCoach, RankedOptions, RankedService, RankedEndpoints, RankedEndpointTests) + edits (FtsDbContext, DependencyInjection, Program, appsettings) — no Unity .meta (server project). **anti-snipe** = a bid with <30s left pushes EndsUtc to now+30s + reschedules; displaces the leader → **outbid FCM** via `INotificationService` + broadcast), CloseWindow (creator force = settle every open lot now — the testable/anti-stall path), SettleDueAsync (Hangfire per-lot entry; BUT counter edge fell short: fast-vs-press 49.2% of decided games (target 55-60%) — confirmed the possession-confound risk: pressing's +midfield and tempo's −midfield self-effects created a possession gap (squared by PossessionSharpness) that drowned the counter. FIX (run 2 pending): possession now moved ONLY by Width; Pressing & Tempo self-effects rerouted to attack/defense; press↔tempo counter made dual (fast side +attack through the press, presser −defense exposed); mentality↔tempo counter moved to defense. Counter sign test now checks Defense (presser) + Attack (fast side). Run 2: counter now 56.0% of decided games ✅ (in 55-60% target, CounterPressVsTempoPercent 6); no-dominant top win rate 53.8% (Def/High/Slow/Wide) < 55% ✅; familiarity unchanged ✅. One over-strict EXTRA assert tripped (worst points share 38.8% < my 0.40 floor — Att/High/Slow/Narrow, a legitimately bad tactic; the ✅ has no floor) → floor relaxed to 0.35 as a broken-tactic tripwire only. Mild accepted asymmetry: defensive tactics edge out attacking ones (defense ratings sit above attack — same known quirk GoalCoefficient compensates). Position stream uses the FINAL active lineups (cosmetic; result never affected). Sim.Core: Match/MatchRule.cs — `ScoreSituation` (Always/Losing/Drawing/Winning/NotWinning/NotLosing, Margin≥1 on Losing/Winning), `Substitution` (OutPlayerId + already-resolved In Player, keeps engine Club-free), `RuleAction` (optional TacticInstructions+InstructionFamiliarity and/or Sub; IsEmpty), `MatchRule` (FromMinute/When/Margin/Action, side-agnostic, ConditionMet(own,opp)), `MatchRuleApplier.Apply(active,rule,isHome,famMax)` (pure: sub rebuilds the side's lineup keeping the out-slot role; instruction change rebuilds the side's TacticContext keeping formation, other side untouched). Rules evaluated at each minute boundary AFTER static plan.Changes, BEFORE the action draw, against score-so-far; fire-once via local bool[] (no hidden state → pure (plan,rules,seed)); a fired rule that yields an invalid input is skipped but marked spent. NO RNG consumed by evaluation → null/empty/never-fire = byte-identical (golden masters safe, no Version bump). Match/PrematchPlan.cs: serializable `PrematchRule`/`PrematchPlan` (ids+enums like LineupPlan/TacticPlan) + `Resolve(club, Func<TacticInstructions,int> familiarityFor, famMax)` → List<MatchRule> (drops empty actions + subs whose In player isn't in the squad). SeasonProgressor.AdvanceDay gained optional 6th arg `IReadOnlyDictionary<int,IReadOnlyList<MatchRule>> rules`; per fixture, a side with rules routes through the rules overload, neither-side-has-rules keeps the exact legacy call (AI-vs-AI byte-identical) — this IS the skipped/unwatched execution path. - **4.1 done (condition model, Sim.Core only — 111 green verified by user; SeasonProgressor/client wiring intentionally deferred, NOT yet done).** Verified harness numbers: form 3-season longest run below 35 = **4** matches (≤6 ✅); fitness→result fresh 517 vs exhausted 318 goals/400 matches ✅; rotation vs fixed XI = fixed 72 pts vs rotated 89 pts over 6×16 congested matches (clear ~24% edge, robust). Pre-existing calibration UNTOUCHED (golden hash 0xCDEA5A2F7B9E5CF6, 2.44 g/m, draws 24.8%, strong 82%, counter 56%, sweep top 53.8%) — proves the opt-in flag default-off is byte-identical. NOTE: first rotation harness (single 14-match block, gap 2d) passed by only 15-vs-14 pts (fragile coin-flip) → hardened to **6 blocks × 16 matches, gap 1 day (truly congested), aggregated points** so the sign is robust; fixedEndFitness sanity tightened to <60. New `Condition` namespace: `Condition/ConditionModel.cs` — pure/deterministic, integer math + one seeded RNG draw for the form walk, no transcendental fns. `PerformanceMultiplier(condition,cfg)` = capped match multiplier, EXACTLY 1.0 at neutral (Form=50/Morale=50/Fitness=100), clamped to [PerformanceFloorPercent/100, PerformanceCapPercent/100]=[0.70,1.08]; terms: form ±FormSwingPermille, morale ±MoraleSwingPermille, fitness −FitnessSwingPermille (fitness only ever reduces, tops at 100=no effect). `ApplyMatchResult(condition,minutes,TeamResult,rng,cfg)` (fitness drain ∝ minutes; form = bounded mean-reverting walk + result nudge if featured; morale += play/bench ± win/loss). `ApplyRest(condition,days,cfg)` (fitness recovery capped 100; morale decays toward neutral, never overshoots). `TeamResult{Loss,Draw,Win}`. BalanceConfig +ConditionBalance section (PerformanceFloor 70/Cap 108; Form/Morale/FitnessSwingPermille 60/50/200; FitnessDrainPer90 24/RecoveryPerDay 3; Form FormReversionPermille 350/RandomStep 5/ResultNudge 3/Neutral 50; Morale Neutral 50/Play 2/Bench 3/Win 4/Loss 4/Decay 1). FEEDING: TeamRatings.FromWithCondition(lineup,cfg) scales each player's rating by the multiplier; MatchEngine ctor gained `bool applyCondition=false` — DEFAULT OFF = byte-identical (golden masters/replays safe, no Version bump), and a neutral squad with the flag ON is ALSO identity (proven by test). Tests: Condition/ConditionTests.cs (10) — neutral=1.0, floor never <70% (20k sweep + worst 0/0/0=0.70), flag-off ignores condition, neutral-squad+flag-on == base engine, tired squad scores less, fitness drain/recovery, morale play/result/decay, **form 3-season mean-reversion (300 players ×114 matches, longest run below 35 ≤6)**, **rotation vs fixed XI over 6 blocks × 16 congested matches (gap 1d), aggregated points: fixed XI craters in fitness & wins fewer pts, rotation stays fresher & wins more** (form/morale pinned neutral in this harness to isolate fatigue). 111 green (101+10); existing 101 untouched (flag defaults off). DESIGN NOTES for review: (a) form params lean gentle (anti-frustration) → form std ~5 around 50, dips below 35 rare/brief; (b) fitness recovery 3/day is calibrated so a weekly cycle of full matches slowly accrues fatigue (rotation matters even at normal cadence); (c) chemistry term in morale is deferred (morale = playing-time+results+decay for now; chemistry/team-talks land with 4.5 support actions); (d) "tired player → sub" pre-match trigger (deferred from 3.5) NOT wired yet — needs a fitness gate on MatchRule; flag if you want it folded in here. SeasonProgressor/client wiring of condition (per-day evolution + opt-in engine flag) NOT done yet — Sim.Core-only task, like 3.2 before 3.3. New .cs files need Unity .meta on first import; build-simcore needed (shared/ changed).
- **4.2 DONE [x] (condition UI + condition made LIVE; user accepted balance, 118 green, golden master unchanged).** Scope decided with user (two questions up front): (1) turn the engine effect ON now (condition pesa sul risultato, validare con harness) — YES; (2) evolve condition for the WHOLE WORLD (not just the user club) — YES. So this deliberately drops the AI-vs-AI byte-identical guarantee for the *live* career (a chosen balance change); the opt-in identity is still proven structurally (see test #1). Sim.Core: `Condition/ConditionProgressor.cs` (new) — pure/deterministic whole-world daily evolution: clubs that played drain their kickoff XI 90' + step the whole squad via ApplyMatchResult, every idle club ApplyRest(1); per-club form RNG = `Pcg32(worldSeed ^ clubId*0x9E37…, day)` so it's order-independent and replay-safe (no transcendental fns, integer math). `SeasonProgressor` ctor gained `bool applyCondition=false` (builds the engine condition-aware) and a new public `EvolveCondition(leagues,season,dayOutcomes,worldSeed,lineupPlans)` that builds per-club Participation (StarterIds via the same ResolveLineup the match used + TeamResult from the score) and delegates to ConditionProgressor. **AdvanceDay stays byte-identical** (evolution is a separate call); existing 101 Sim.Core tests untouched. CRITICAL ORDERING for the watched user match (3.4): AdvanceDay simulates on the pre-match condition, THEN EvolveCondition mutates it — so by the time the user watches, the live players are already drained. Fix: LocalClock captures a **kickoff condition snapshot** (both squads, clones of Form/Morale/Fitness) into `UserMatchContext.KickoffCondition` *before* calling EvolveCondition; MatchWatchScreenPresenter's re-sim (`ResimWithKickoffCondition`) restores those values for the duration of the sim (finally-restores the live evolved values), and its engine is now `new MatchEngine(applyCondition:true)`. Without this the watch re-sim prefix would diverge from the committed result. Client: LocalClock `_progressor = new SeasonProgressor(applyCondition:true)` + calls EvolveCondition each day after the snapshot; UserMatchContext +KickoffCondition (NOT persisted, regenerable); CareerState UNCHANGED — PlayerCondition (Form/Morale/Fitness) was already serialized, so no save-version bump. NOTE (corrected after the test run): generation does NOT start players neutral — Form=50 but Morale∈[50,70] and Fitness∈[95,100] (GenerationBalance), so fresh squads carry a small net condition effect and the live career diverges from the pre-condition engine from **day 1** (the deliberate effect, not identity); old v5 saves likewise carry their generated condition and diverge immediately on load. The identity guarantee is purely structural: forced-neutral squad + flag on == flag off (test #1). UI (the actual 4.2 ✅): `Presenters/ConditionFormatter.cs` (ConditionDisplay.Build → form arrow ▲/▬/▼, morale face :)/:|/:(, fitness 0-100, and a tooltip that ALWAYS states the cause per band); SquadView rows rebuilt as clickable VisualElements with a condition strip (fitness bar painted green/amber/red + form arrow + morale face) and `row.tooltip` = the why-text; SquadScreenPresenter fills the new VM fields for slots + roster. Loc: full `condition.*` block (3 tooltip formats + fitness/form/morale cause phrases) en+it at parity (17 keys each). Glyphs (▲▼ ●-family, ASCII faces) are iconography, not translatable text. Tests added: `Career/SeasonProgressorConditionTests.cs` (4) — (1) engine-flag-on + **forced-neutral** squad == legacy byte-identical over a full season (opt-in safety; initially written assuming neutral generation, which was WRONG → now pins neutral explicitly), (2) a full LIVE season replays deterministically incl. final per-player condition, (3) lifecycle: starters drain on a matchday & recover on rest (prints the numbers), (4) **calibration harness**: full live season prints goals/match, draw%, home/away win%, scoreline histogram + asserts a loose band [2.0,3.0] g/m & [15,38]% draws. **Run 1: 114/115 green — only test #1 failed (fixture 60 score flip) because generation randomizes morale/fitness so fresh squads aren't neutral; fixed by pinning neutral. Run 2 (user): 115/115 green ✅.** BALANCE DECISION: user accepted the 2.55 g/m (vs 2.44 baseline) — a web search confirmed fatigue lowers shooting accuracy/speed and good condition lifts attacking output, so "manage condition well → score more" is realistic and aligned with the design pillar; the small +0.11 league-average uplift (partly the generated morale floor of 50) is accepted. Condition is now LIVE and accepted. HARNESS NUMBERS (run 1): `[condition-live calibration]` 380 matches **2.55 g/m**, draws **24.2%**, home wins **45.5%**, away **30.3%** vs pre-condition baseline 2.44 g/m / 24.8% / home 48.4% — i.e. +0.11 g/m and home edge softened ~3pp (more per-match variance lets favourites slip slightly). `[lifecycle]` starters 76.0 → 85.0 fitness (drain then recover). BALANCE VERDICT (for the user to accept or tune): condition is ~scale-invariant when both sides tire equally, so the shift is small and stays in the accepted band — I'd accept it, but the generated morale floor of 50 (only ever ≥ neutral) is a candidate to re-centre later if we want exact baseline parity. DESIGN NOTES for review: (a) subs' partial minutes not modelled — a starter is credited a full 90', the rest 0' (kickoff XI only); (b) an intervened (subbed) watch result is re-committed but condition was already evolved on the kickoff XI assumption (minor, documented); (c) AI clubs field BestEleven every match (no rotation yet) so their regulars fatigue across a season — harmless to calibration per (a)'s scale-invariance, but means AI doesn't *exploit* rotation (AI rotation is a later/market-phase concern); (d) condition shown on the Squad screen AND the in-match pause panel (user-requested) via a shared `Views/ConditionStrip` helper; the pause panel reads the KICKOFF snapshot (the model has no within-match fatigue curve, so the value a player took into the game is what matters for a sub) — the tactics opponent panel could show it later. HANDOFF: shared/ changed → run `.\tools\build-simcore.ps1`, then `dotnet test … --logger "console;verbosity=detailed"` and paste the `[condition-live calibration]` + `[lifecycle]` lines so we judge the band together; new .cs files (ConditionProgressor, ConditionFormatter, SeasonProgressorConditionTests) need Unity .meta on first import; then Play-mode check the Squad screen bars/tooltips + that benching a tired player recovers his bar. Keep 4.2 at [~] until verified.
- **4.2 REFINEMENT (user-requested, Sim.Core changed → NOT yet built/harnessed): uniform starting fitness + stamina-driven drain.** (1) Generation now starts EVERY player at full fitness (`AttributeScale.MaxCondition`) so the bars are equal at career start; the random fitness draw is KEPT and discarded so the generation RNG stream stays byte-identical (golden masters safe — they're flag-off so fitness value is irrelevant anyway). (2) `ConditionModel.ApplyMatchResult` gained an optional `int stamina = 50` (neutral pivot) and a `StaminaScaledDrain` helper: drain = baseDrain × (1000 + (StaminaNeutral − stamina) × StaminaDrainSwingPermille / StaminaNeutral) / 1000, clamped ≥0 — low stamina tires faster, high slower, stamina=50 = exact identity (so the 4.1 tests and any default caller stay byte-identical). BalanceConfig.Condition += `StaminaNeutral=50`, `StaminaDrainSwingPermille=400` (±40% at the stamina extremes). `ConditionProgressor.ApplyMatch` passes `player.Attributes.Stamina` (already a generated 1-100 attribute, varies by role+noise). So differentiation now comes from how fast players tire, not from a random starting value — the user's intent. HANDOFF: build-simcore + dotnet test, paste `[condition-live calibration]` + `[lifecycle]` again (avg stamina ~50 keeps avg drain ~24, so league calibration should barely move; StaminaDrainSwingPermille is the tuning knob). **WITHIN-MATCH FATIGUE (user chose "incide sul risultato", engine-affecting): built, 118/118 green, harness run done.** New engine flag `MatchEngine(…, bool applyMatchFatigue=false)` — separate from applyCondition so golden masters and the flag-off / neutral-condition identity are untouched. Model: ratings are now recomputed EVERY minute (was: only on input change; recompute is byte-identical when the flag is off — identical values, no RNG consumed — so existing goldens hold); when on, each side's ratings are scaled by a per-side `FatigueFactor(minute, avgStamina)` = tiredness building linearly to `MatchFatigueAt90Permille` (80=−8% for a neutral-stamina XI) by full time, stepped back by `HalfTimeRecoveryPermille` (20=+2%) after minute 45 (so the 2nd half restarts fresher than the close of the 1st — the user's ask), then scaled by the on-pitch XI's AVERAGE stamina ((2·StaminaNeutral−avgStamina)/StaminaNeutral: low-stamina XI fades up to ~2×, a 100-stamina XI doesn't fade). KEY: equal-stamina sides fade equally → scale-invariant → league goals/match barely move; the effect is RELATIVE — the fresher/higher-stamina side (or one that subs on fresh legs, which lifts avgStamina) gains the edge, especially late. Config: BalanceConfig.Condition += `MatchFatigueAt90Permille=80`, `HalfTimeRecoveryPermille=20`. Threaded through `SeasonProgressor(…, bool applyMatchFatigue=false)`; client LIVE on both fronts — `LocalClock` SeasonProgressor and `MatchWatchScreenPresenter` engine both `applyMatchFatigue:true`. Tests: `Condition/MatchFatigueTests.cs` (3) — flag-off == base engine byte-identical (proves the per-minute recompute is safe), determinism per seed, and the relative-edge harness (high-stamina 95 vs low-stamina 10, 400 seeds, fatigue-on net GF-GA > fatigue-off net; the stamina rating-confound cancels in the on−off delta). `SeasonProgressorConditionTests` live runs now pass `applyMatchFatigue:true` (calibration/determinism/lifecycle); the identity test keeps fatigue OFF. DESIGN LIMITATIONS (v1, documented): fatigue is per-SIDE on avg stamina, not per-player on-pitch minutes — so a fresh sub helps via the side average rather than being individually tracked (good enough; per-player entry-time tracking deferred). HARNESS RESULTS (118/118 green; golden master 0xCDEA…UNCHANGED ✓): `[match-fatigue]` high-stamina net GF-GA over 400 = off **481** → on **635** (+154, big & correct-signed ✓). `[lifecycle]` 81.1 → 90.1 (drain now ~19 not 24 because starters' avg stamina <50 lifts endurance vs the old flat 24; still drains+recovers ✓). `[condition-live calibration]` 380 matches **2.60 g/m**, draws **20.5%**, home 47.6%, away 31.8% — vs the pre-fatigue 4.2 number 2.55 g/m / 24.2% draws, and pre-condition baseline 2.44 / 24.8%. So within-match fatigue is roughly goals-neutral (+0.05 g/m) BUT drops DRAWS ~3.7pp (24.2→20.5): the fresher/higher-stamina side pulling away late makes games more decisive. 20.5% is still in-band but below the historic ~25% target. BALANCE DECISION (user): softened `MatchFatigueAt90Permille` **80 → 60** (HalfTimeRecoveryPermille kept 20). Re-run (118/118 green, golden master 0xCDEA…unchanged): `[condition-live calibration]` **2.59 g/m, draws 21.3%** (only +0.8pp vs the 80 run — the decisiveness is structural: within-match fatigue rewards the fresher/higher-stamina side, so as soon as stamina differs draws fall ~3pp regardless of magnitude; softening further recovers little for a weaker effect), home 47.4%, away 31.3%; `[match-fatigue]` off 481 → on **580** (still clearly correct-signed). RECOMMENDATION to user: accept 60/21.3% (realistic, effect meaningful). **ACCEPTED by user — 4.2 closed [x]** (user signed off on the balance; Play-mode of the Squad + pause-panel condition strips is the user's to spot-check as usual).
- **4.3 DONE [x] (training system — harness VERIFIED 124 green by user, golden master unchanged; user closed it on the ✅. Client Training-screen + live wiring WRITTEN, Play-mode spot-check still the user's as usual — client can't be built here).** HARNESS NUMBERS (user run, 124/124 green, golden master 0xCDEA…UNCHANGED ✓): `[training-diverge]` attacking shoot+drib **2828** vs defending **2669**; defending defend **1586** vs attacking **1501** (correct-signed, ~3-4 pts/player in the focused area over a season with +20 headroom & shared RNG); `[training-decline]` 21/22 at-potential players lost ground over 2 seasons, capped at −8; `[training-world]` AI club skill-total +150/season (Balanced default; youth grow, veterans decline → world not frozen, mildly net-positive). User VERDICT: gentle/realistic movement accepted as-is; `GrowthPerMillePerWeight` (130) is the knob if training should feel heavier; the −8 decline cap stays mild until the 4.4 age curve. Scope decided with user (3 questions up front): (1) Sim.Core + harness first, screen + live wiring after; (2) the user controls ONLY his own club's training, but the WHOLE WORLD's players still improve/decline (AI clubs train a Balanced default); (3) focuses are by attribute GROUP (not single attribute). New `Development` namespace (was empty): `Development/TrainingFocus.cs` — `TeamTrainingFocus {Balanced,Attacking,Defending,Physical,Technical,Tactical}` + `IndividualTrainingFocus {None,Attacking,Defending,Physical,Technical}` (stable numeric values = save keys). `Development/TrainingPlan.cs` — serializable (TeamFocus + Dictionary<int,IndividualTrainingFocus> IndividualFocuses, FocusFor(id), Balanced()), mirrors LineupPlan/TacticPlan; host owns persistence. `Development/TrainingModel.cs` — PURE/deterministic, integer math + exactly `PlayerAttributes.SkillCount`(=10) RNG draws per player per week (one per skill, BOTH regimes, so changing focus re-thresholds the SAME rolls → isolates the effect in A/B harnesses; no transcendental fns). Two private weight tables (TeamWeights[6,10], IndividualWeights[5,10], same skill order as PlayerRating); `CombinedWeights(team,indiv)` = team+individual per skill. `ApplyTrainingWeek(player,team,indiv,rng,cfg)`: gap = Potential − Overall; gap>0 → **Grow** (per-skill +1 with prob = weight·GrowthPerMillePerWeight/100 · headroom-scale, clamped to GrowthMaxPerMille; gate recomputes Overall per increment so growth STOPS EXACTLY AT Potential — strict ceiling, slows near it); gap≤0 → **Decline** (each skill −1 at DeclinePerMille, but only while Overall stays ≥ Potential−DeclineFloorPoints (capped, anti-frustration), and a skill whose combined weight ≥ DeclineProtectionWeightThreshold declines slower — drilling keeps it sharp). `TacticalFamiliarityGain(team,cfg)` = the "affects tactic familiarity" half: Tactical focus → TacticalFocusFamiliarityGainPerWeek, every other focus → 0 (host adds it to the stored level via FamiliarityModel). `Development/TrainingProgressor.cs` — whole-world weekly evolution (like ConditionProgressor): `EvolveWeek(leagues, plans, worldSeed, week)`; user club uses its plan, every other club uses TrainingPlan.Balanced (AI default); per-club RNG = `Pcg32(worldSeed ^ clubId·0x9E37…, week)` → order-independent, replay-safe. Domain: added a per-skill INDEXER `PlayerAttributes[int]` (+ const SkillCount) in canonical skill order (clamping setters) so systems iterate skills generically — reused by TrainingModel, handy for 4.4. New `Condition` files (all pure/deterministic, **integer math, NO RNG**, no transcendental fns; opt-in by being called → the match engine & SeasonProgressor never reference them, so golden master 0xCDEA…/replays & the existing 130 tests are UNAFFECTED, no Version bump): `Condition/SupportAction.cs` — `SupportAction` enum (Praise=0/Encourage=1/Motivate=2/Criticize=3/Rest=4, stable numeric save keys) + `SupportOutcome` readonly struct (Applied + Morale/Form/Fitness deltas + SkipsTraining); `Condition/SupportActionLog.cs` — per-(playerId,action) last-used-day store with `IsOnCooldown`/`DaysUntilAvailable`/`Record` + `Export`/`Import` for host persistence (in-memory, host-owned like the tactic familiarity log; `CooldownDays(action,cfg)` static helper); `Condition/SupportActionModel.cs` — `Resolve(action,condition,cfg)` (PURE, no mutation, no cooldown: returns the intended deltas; magnitudes from config, the "when does it land" context patterns are STRUCTURAL in code like the tactics counter-matrix) + `TryApply(condition,action,log,playerId,currentDay,cfg)` (cooldown-gated: blocked → `SupportOutcome.Blocked` no-op; else mutates via the 0..100-clamping PlayerCondition setters, records the day, returns ACTUAL post-clamp deltas). CONTEXT (structural, permille integer, all capped by the 0..100 clamp): Praise = morale headroom × form-fit (full at neutral morale+form, **0 at the morale ceiling** and ~0 after a poor run → repeated praise saturates to nothing); Encourage = strongest at low morale, linear to 0 at the ceiling; Motivate = peaks at neutral morale (maxed have nothing to prove, broken need encouragement first), small +morale **and +form**; Criticize = **−morale that lands harder on the fragile (low morale) and is shrugged off by the confident**, plus a +form "wake-up" spark the confident convert and the fragile barely do (so criticising a happy in-form star is a useful kick, criticising someone who's down is a bounded telegraphed mistake); Rest = +fitness scaled by tiredness (0 when already fresh) + small flat +morale + **SkipsTraining=true** (host honours the training opportunity cost in its dev tick). `Config/BalanceConfig.cs` += `SupportBalance Support` section (morale magnitudes Praise 8/Encourage 10/Motivate 5/Criticize 8/Rest 3; secondary MotivateFormNudge 3/CriticizeFormSpark 4/RestFitnessRecovery 15; cooldown days Praise/Encourage/Motivate 14, Criticize/Rest 21). Tests: `Sim.Core.Tests/Condition/SupportActionTests.cs` (10) — (1) each action moves condition in the configured direction at a favourable context; (2) **magnitudes == config at full context** (Encourage@morale0=10, Praise@50/50=8, Motivate@50=5morale+3form, Rest@fit0=15); (3) Praise saturates to **0** at the morale ceiling; (4) Praise rings hollow out of form; (5) Encourage stronger the lower the morale; (6) Criticize hurts the fragile more + sparks the confident; (7) Rest recovers less when fresh (0 at full fitness); (8) **THE ✅ cooldown** — first apply lands, a re-use inside the cooldown is **blocked + moves nothing**, then it's available again exactly at cooldownDays; (9) cooldowns are independent per-action and per-player; (10) determinism (no RNG). **VERIFIED: 140 green (130+10), all 10 support tests pass, golden master 0xCDEA5A2F7B9E5CF6 unchanged**; existing 130 untouched. DESIGN NOTES for review: (a) magnitudes are first-pass tunables — context curves are structural so the comparative asserts hold regardless of exact calibration; eyeball whether the moves feel right (the *MoraleBoost/Hit knobs); (b) `TryApply` records the use (starts the cooldown) even when the contextual effect is ~0 — using an action spends it, the context just means it was wasted (intended); (c) Rest's SkipsTraining is exposed for the host; Sim.Core never schedules training; (d) no RNG by design → trivially replay-safe and the 4.5 actions can't perturb any seeded stream. New screen: `Views/SupportView.cs` (dumb — selectable roster rows reusing the 4.2 ConditionStrip + tooltip via ConditionDisplay, a row of 5 action buttons that grey out via SetEnabled when on cooldown, a help line + status line; strings+ids only, no Sim.Core ref) and `Presenters/SupportScreenPresenter.cs` (rehydrates a Sim.Core `SupportActionLog` from `SupportCooldowns` on Enter; an action click calls `SupportActionModel.TryApply(player.Condition, action, log, playerId, Season.CurrentDay, cfg)` — `new BalanceConfig().Support`; on success writes the log back via `Export()`→dict, saves, shows a signed delta line "name — morale +6, form +2"; a Rest also adds the player to `RestedSinceTraining`). Resolved via `Push<SupportScreenPresenter>` (ctor deps ScreenNavigator/CareerState/ISaveRepository/ILocalizationService all already in scope — no DI edit). New `Market` namespace (was empty): `Market/ValuationModel.cs` — PURE/deterministic, **integer/long math, NO RNG**, no transcendental fns (so identical on .NET/Mono/IL2CPP and can't perturb any seeded stream); `Value(player, leagueLevel, cfg) → long` (+ a `leagueLevel=1` overload) and `Reprice(player, leagueLevel, cfg)` (writes MarketValue). Shape (magnitudes in config, structure in code like TacticModifiers/AgeCurve): (1) **valueRating** = overall + a youth-scaled share of remaining potential headroom — the youth scaling REUSES `AgeCurve.GrowthFactorPermille` (1000 teen → 0 at the role peak), so a young prospect prices toward his ceiling and a peaked player gets no premium (this is the "young star > equal-ability 30yo" mechanic); (2) convex **base** = `ValueUnitPerRatingCubed × (valueRating − RatingValueFloor)^ValueExponent` (cubic → top talents cost far more); (3) permille **multipliers** for age (resale fades after the prime, separate `ValueDeclineOnsetAge` 29 from the dev-decline onset), short-term form (small ±4%), contract length (expiring deal discounts the fee), league level (lower divisions discount); (4) clamp to `[MinValue 25k, MaxValue 250M]` + round to `ValueRoundingUnit` 5k → always positive, never absurd. `Market/ValuationProgressor.cs` — whole-world re-pricing: walks leagues, writes each player's MarketValue at that league's `Division` level; pure/deterministic, NO RNG, order-independent; opt-in by being called (host owns cadence). Domain: `Player.MarketValue` (long, additive — defaults 0, save-compatible, 0 until first priced). `Config/BalanceConfig.cs` += `MarketBalance Market` (ValueUnitPerRatingCubed 230 / RatingValueFloor 30 / ValueExponent 3 / ValueRatingCap 110; PotentialWeightPercent 60; ValueDeclineOnsetAge 29 / ValueDeclinePerMillePerYear 90 / ValueAgeFloorPermille 250; FormValueSwingPermille 40; ContractFullSeasons 3 / ContractExpiringFloorPermille 350; LeagueLevelDiscountPermille 150 / LeagueLevelFloorPermille 400; MinValue 25k / MaxValue 250M / ValueRoundingUnit 5k; **ELITE TAIL (added after the first run, user wanted phenoms à la Transfermarkt — top market values €180-234M, stars ~€90M, masses €5-50M): EliteValueThreshold 72 / ElitePremiumPermillePerPoint 110** — no premium below the threshold, then +11% per value-rating point above it, so the rare elite vr (a top star or a young high-potential prospect) explodes toward the €100-250M cap while the masses are untouched; cubic base alone topped out ~€60M because our overall scale is compressed (top ~76)). Scale calibration (predicted): a prime 70-overall (no headroom) ≈ €14.7M, 80 ≈ €28.75M, 90 ≈ €49.7M, squad filler (40) ≈ €230k; the 19yo pot-92 striker ≈ €40.4M vs a 30yo same-overall ≈ €23.1M. NOTHING in the engine/SeasonProgressor calls valuation and the engine never reads MarketValue → golden master/replays & the existing 140 tests UNAFFECTED (opt-in, like the condition/development progressors); no Version bump. All asserts are deterministic integer computations (the guardrails are structural clamps) — NONE are statistically fragile. Decisions (2 questions answered): retune Sim.Core balance AND polish the client flow + all four UI areas. (1) **Sim.Core** `Config/BalanceConfig.cs` TransferBalance: `StarterAskPremillePermille` 1500→**1250** and `StarterMinSalePermille` 1300→**1150** — at 150% the starter ask pushed a fair-value offer UNDER the 60%-of-ask lowball band, so the user only ever saw rejections; at 125% a near-value offer now lands in the COUNTER band (the "devono esserci controfferte" ask) and starters cost ~115-125% of value instead of 130-150%. Floor still well under BuyerMaxValuePermille 1450 so a wanted starter stays reachable; the AI window self-limits, hard cap MaxSigningsPerClubPerWindow 3 × 40 = 120 < the 150 test ceiling. The 162 TransferTests recompute every floor/ask from cfg or use offsets relative to SellerWalkAwayPermille, so they stay green (RejectsALowball derives the lowball from walkaway; AcceptsAfterACounter uses Squad importance + an 80%-of-ask offer, unaffected); volume may tick up from ~50 but stays in the 45-150 band — JUDGE from the reprinted `[transfers-window]`/`[transfers-nopeanuts]` lines. (2) **Client negotiation flow** (`NegotiationScreenPresenter`, NO Sim.Core dep beyond the step API): a lowball `EvaluateOffer→Reject` (buy) or `RespondToCounter→GiveUp` (sell) now BREAKS OFF the talks (`_brokenOff`, all actions disabled) — "si spazientisce e interrompe"; an explicit patience limiter = MaxNegotiationRounds offers, shown live as `negotiation.offers_left`, and running out ends the talks (`negotiation.status.ended`); "Accept ask/offer" (capitulate) stays available until broken-off and does NOT spend patience; clearer status messages for counter/raise/broke_off/ended/agreed. (3) **UI** (`NegotiationView` rebuilt): the amount is now a TYPEABLE TextField (digit-filtered, SetValueWithoutNotify so the presenter's updates don't fight typing) PLUS −/+ with a PROPORTIONAL step (~5% of the current amount, min €25k), a formatted "€x.xM" hint under it, and the offers-left line; info in a card. `MarketView`: budget split from the window banner, banner colour-coded (green open / amber closed). MoveUser: the club he leaves gets a fresh AI coach, the user takes the new club's bench carrying his reputation (confidence reset), UserClubId updated, squad-specific prefs cleared (lineup/plan/training/minutes/rested/listings) while tactic knowledge + watch lists travel. Both views dumb (no Sim.Core ref, verified). CareerState += SeasonEvaluated + CareerHistory (new `CareerHistoryEntry` client type); coach rep/confidence/objective/last-finish ride Club.Coach. CareerFactory seeds the coach world + marks the user human at creation. GameSessionService registers CareerService. NOTE: first run failed — the initial competence model only SHUFFLED players between slots (the overlooked starter re-entered at another role → XI unchanged, Normal==Hard, avg 78==78); fixed so an overlooked starter is BENCHED for the whole match (excluded from the XI, with a guard that never drops below 11), which genuinely weakens the side. AiLineupCompetence is the knob if the spread needs tuning. New `Difficulty` namespace (pure/deterministic, integer math, NO RNG of its own — the only randomness is the per-fixture AI-lineup RNG the host seeds; opt-in → match engine never reads it, golden master/replays safe, no Version/save bump): `DifficultyLevel` (Easy=0/Normal=1/Hard=2, stable save keys); `DifficultySettings` (resolved knobs) + `DifficultyContext` (the AI-competence slice the season needs: HumanClubId + competence + maxSlips); `DifficultyModel` (PURE — `Resolve(level,cfg)`→settings; `MatchContext(humanClubId,settings)`; `SeedBudgets(leagues,humanClubId,settings,cfg)` seeds via BudgetModel then scales the USER club by UserBudgetPermille [generous on Easy] and every AI club by AiBudgetPermille [aggressive on Hard], floored; `ApplyBoardPatience(cfg,settings)` scales CareerBalance.Confidence*PerPositionVsObjective IN PLACE — leaving the thresholds + MaxConfidenceDeltaPerEvaluation untouched so the 5.6 "warning before sacking" invariant survives; call ONCE per career config). `Config/BalanceConfig.cs` += `DifficultyBalance Difficulty` (per-level rows indexed 0/1/2: **AiLineupCompetence {55,78,100}** [percent chance an AI fields the best player per slot; 100 = always best XI = the pre-5.7 AI; Normal 78 is deliberately <100 so all three levels separate], AiLineupMaxSlips 3, **UserBudgetPermille {1500,1000,700}**, **AiBudgetPermille {750,1000,1300}**, **BoardReactivityPermille {700,1000,1400}**). `Match/LineupSelector.cs` += `CompetentEleven(club,formation,competencePercent,maxSlips,rng)` — per slot, ranks unused candidates best-first (rating desc, id tie-break) and slips down a rank each time a competence roll misses (≤ maxSlips); at competence ≥100 NO roll can miss → returns exactly BestEleven (so Hard is byte-identical to legacy). `Career/SeasonProgressor.cs` threaded an optional `DifficultyContext? difficulty=null` through AdvanceDay (both overloads) / Simulate / ResolveLineup / EvolveCondition — when set, an AI club (≠ HumanClubId, competence <100) with no explicit LineupPlan fields a CompetentEleven via a DEDICATED per-(fixture,club) `LineupRng` decorrelated from the match RNG (so a degraded XI never perturbs the seeded match draw); default null = byte-identical pre-5.7 path (the user's club is always best-XI). Tests `Sim.Core.Tests/Difficulty/DifficultyTests.cs` (5): **THE ✅ `WinRate_SeparatesClearly...`** — 8 seeds × full season, the user club (index 9) fields best XI throughout (identical behaviour) while AI competence = the level; sums user points and asserts Easy > Normal > Hard (Easy−Hard ≥ 8), prints `[difficulty-winrate]` per-seed + totals; `HardDifficulty_...ByteIdentically` (Hard/competence-100 reproduces the legacy season fixture-for-fixture — proves the opt-in identity); `CompetentEleven_AtFullCompetence_EqualsBestEleven_AndIsDeterministic` (==BestEleven at 100, same seed→same XI, low competence→weaker avg, prints `[difficulty-competence]`); `Budgets_FavourTheUserOnEasy_AndTheAiOnHard` (prints `[difficulty-budget]`); `BoardPatience_SacksFasterOnHard_ButStillWarnsFirst...` (mild persistent underachievement → Hard sacks sooner than Easy AND Warning−MaxDelta≥Sack holds at every level, prints `[difficulty-board]`). DESIGN NOTES for review: (a) magnitudes are first-pass — the ✅ asserts a comparative inequality so it holds regardless of exact calibration; eyeball `[difficulty-winrate]` and tell me if the Easy/Normal/Hard spread feels right (AiLineupCompetence is the knob); (b) Hard = competence 100 = the AI as it played before 5.7, so Normal/Easy are softer-than-today by design (difficulty is being INTRODUCED, Normal is the new intended default); (c) board patience only bites for SMALL position gaps (large gaps hit the per-evaluation cap on every level by design) — that's why the harness uses a 2-position miss; (d) AI market aggressiveness is expressed purely as the AI budget multiplier for now (5.2 already proved budget is the throttle on signings); deeper AI need-threshold scaling is a later option.
