- **6.1 DONE [x] (art pass — Sim.Core 6.1a VERIFIED & ACCEPTED: 199/199 green, golden master 0xCDEA5A2F7B9E5CF6 UNCHANGED; client 6.1b WRITTEN & Play-mode ACCEPTED by the user). 🏁 Phase 6 started.** Decisions with user (3 up front): Sim.Core deterministic identity generator + harness first then client; procedural painter2D crest; full USS design-token theme + restyle of existing screens. **6.1a:** new `Identity` namespace (pure/deterministic, integer math, NO transcendental fns; the only RNG is a local `Pcg32` derived from (clubId, worldSeed) like `ClubPersonalities.For` → replay-safe, consumes no shared stream; the match engine never calls it → golden master/replays UNAFFECTED, no Version/save bump — identity is regenerated on demand, never stored): `RgbColor` (readonly struct, integer HSV→RGB six-sector, Rec.601 luminance, L1 colour distance, Packed 0xRRGGBB + ToHex for USS), `ClubColors` (Primary/Secondary/Accent/Neutral), `CrestDesign` (`CrestShape` {Shield,Circle,Diamond,RoundedSquare} + `CrestPattern` {Solid,VHalves,HHalves,Sash,VStripes,Hoops,Quarters} + render-ready struct shape+pattern+FillA/FillB/Trim/Emblem), `ClubIdentity`, `ClubIdentityGenerator.Generate(clubId, worldSeed, IdentityBalance)` (vivid primary; secondary via a weighted harmony scheme {complementary/analogous±/triadic/neutral-contrast}; metallic accent; crest shape/pattern). TWO structural readability guarantees: (1) Primary↔Secondary always ≥ `MinFillColorDistance` apart (a too-close hued secondary is replaced by the contrast neutral) so a two-tone kit/crest never collapses to one colour; (2) Emblem/Neutral = the light-or-dark neutral that contrasts the primary, so initials are always legible. `Config/BalanceConfig.cs` += `IdentityBalance` (primary/secondary sat-val ranges, harmony hue offsets + `SchemeWeights`, `NeutralLight`/`NeutralDark`, `AccentColors` {gold/silver/white/near-black}, `ContrastLuminanceThreshold` 140, `MinFillColorDistance` 120, `ShapeWeights`/`PatternWeights`). Tests `Sim.Core.Tests/Identity/IdentityTests.cs` (9). HARNESS (accepted): `[identity-spread]` 1000/1000 distinct primaries; `[identity-fills]` worst fill distance 120 (= floor, holds); `[identity-legible]` worst emblem contrast 106 (≥100); `[identity-crest]` shapes 377/320/124/179, patterns 217/172/122/163/146/96/84 (all reachable). Variety accepted; knobs: MinFillColorDistance, ShapeWeights/PatternWeights, sat/val ranges. **6.1b (client, NO Sim.Core change → 199 green + golden master stand):** (1) **Central reskin** — `Views/UiKit.cs` rewritten from the 2.1 placeholder helper into a cartoon design-token theme (palette Background/Surface/SurfaceAlt/Border + Accent emerald/AccentDark/Amber + Text/Positive/Warning/Danger; type scale; spacing/radius tokens; factories Screen/Card/Row/Divider/Title/Header/Subtitle/Caption/MenuButton[rounded pill]/PrimaryButton/Pill/ProgressBar + Round/SetBorder/Hex). Legacy members (MenuGreen/HubBlue→Background/PanelGray→Surface/Screen/Title/Subtitle/MenuButton) KEEP names+signatures so all 20 screens compile unchanged and adopt the look centrally (= the "restyle schermate"). (2) **`Views/CrestRenderer.cs`** — painter2D `VisualElement`, primitives only (shape int 0-3, pattern int 0-6, Unity fill/trim/emblem colours, mask colour = the surface it sits on, initials) → NO Sim.Core ref, stays in FTS.Views. Paints the two-tone pattern across the crest box then clips to the silhouette with an even-odd FRAME MASK (outer rect + inner shape filled `Painter2D.Fill(FillRule.OddEven)` in the surface colour → outside-shape repainted as bg, no real clip region), a trim stroke + initials on top. RoundedSquare renders as a square box in v1 (rounded corners deferred to avoid blind ArcTo risk). (3) **Identity bridge** — `Services/ClubVisual.cs` (readonly struct: crest ints + Unity colours) + `Services/ClubIdentityService.cs` (Game scope; wraps the pure Sim.Core generator with `CareerState.Seed`, caches per club, `RgbColor`→Unity `Color`; `Visual(clubId)`/`UserVisual()`), registered in `GameSessionService` Game scope. NO save bump. (4) **Hub flagship** — `HubView` gained a crest slot + a primary-colour accent underline (dumb: takes a `VisualElement` + a `Color`); `HubPresenter` injects `ClubIdentityService`, builds a 96px `CrestRenderer` from `UserVisual()`+`club.ShortName` (mask `UiKit.Background`) and tints the hub with the club primary. No manual `new HubPresenter(` call sites (resolved via `Push<HubPresenter>` → DI fills the new param). User ran the WebGL build, served it over HTTP (localhost:8000) and confirmed in-browser: full Hub/career loads, branded loading screen, save persists, `DeterminismCheck` logged 0xCDEA5A2F7B9E5CF6. Closed after the post-playtest scroll fix (see end of this entry); FM-style visual redesign queued as 6.6–6.8.** Decisions up front: hosting "non so ancora" → **Brotli + decompression fallback** (gira ovunque, niente config header server); **schermata di caricamento custom brandizzata**; **modifica diretta di ProjectSettings + link.xml**. (1) **IndexedDB save persistence:** `client/Assets/Plugins/WebGL/FtsSaveSync.jslib` (mergeInto → `FS.syncfs(false,…)`) + `Services/Persistence/WebGLSaveSync.cs` (`[DllImport("__Internal")] FtsSyncFilesystem` under `#if UNITY_WEBGL && !UNITY_EDITOR`, no-op off WebGL); `LocalJsonSaveRepository.Save()` and `Delete()` call `WebGLSaveSync.Flush()` after the atomic move/delete so a tab close/refresh can't lose a career (Unity auto-syncs IDBFS→persistentDataPath at startup, so load is fine). (2) **`client/Assets/link.xml`** preserves `Newtonsoft.Json` + `Sim.Core` + `Fts.Contracts` + `FTS.Services` whole — IL2CPP managed stripping (now Low) would otherwise strip reflection-only serialized types/ctors and silently break save/load in the stripped player only (works in Editor → hard to catch). (3) **Custom template** `client/Assets/WebGLTemplates/FTS/index.html` — self-contained (inline CSS, zero image assets) cartoon loader: UiKit palette (#141C30 bg / #27C281 accent / #F2B33D amber), CSS/SVG-drawn shield, progress bar fed by the `createUnityInstance(progress)` callback, error/warning banner via `showBanner`, mobile devicePixelRatio=1 hint; Unity-6 macros incl. `#if MEMORY_FILENAME/SYMBOLS_FILENAME`. (4) **ProjectSettings.asset** (direct YAML edits): productName "Football Team Simulator", companyName "Fabio Casarini", `webGLCompressionFormat: 0` (Brotli — enum verified Brotli=0/Gzip=1/Disabled=2 via Unity docs), `webGLDecompressionFallback: 1`, `webGLTemplate: PROJECT:FTS`, `managedStrippingLevel:` → `WebGL: 1` (Low); kept `webGLExceptionSupport: 1` (explicitly-thrown-only, small/fast) + `webGLDataCaching: 1`. (5) **Headless build:** `client/Assets/Editor/WebGLBuilder.cs` (predefined Editor asm; `-executeMethod Fts.EditorTools.WebGLBuilder.Build`, builds the enabled Build-Settings scenes to `-buildOutput`, `EditorApplication.Exit(1)` on failure, logs total size) + `tools/build-webgl.ps1` (reads ProjectVersion, auto-detects `Unity.exe` for 6000.3.17f1 under Hub paths or `-UnityPath`, `-quit -batchmode -nographics`, log to `client/builds/webgl-build.log`, reminds to serve over HTTP). `builds/` is gitignored. (6) **Performance pass:** audited hot paths — MatchRenderer playback is scheduler-driven (`schedule.Execute(OnPump).Every`), `OnGenerateVisualContent` uses `Vector2` structs (stack, no GC) with the smoothing arrays built once at setup; AppEntryPoint.Tick only polls Input. No per-frame heap allocations → no code change; the size/perf levers ARE the Brotli + Low-stripping + link.xml combo. (2) **Build tooling:** `client/Assets/Editor/MobileBuilder.cs` (`BuildAndroid` — APK default, `-aab` for App Bundle via `EditorUserBuildSettings.buildAppBundle`; `BuildiOS` — exports an Xcode project, Mac-only to turn into an .ipa; mirrors WebGLBuilder, exits 1 on failure) + `tools/build-android.ps1` (`-Aab`/`-UnityPath`/`-Output`, auto-detects Unity 6000.3.17f1, `-executeMethod Fts.EditorTools.MobileBuilder.BuildAndroid`, log to `client/builds/android-build.log`, prints the `adb install -r` hint). (3) **Safe area:** `Services/SafeAreaController.cs` (App-scope `IStartable`+`ITickable` entry point) pads the UIDocument root by `Screen.safeArea` converted pixels→points via the resolved panel size, re-applying on `GeometryChangedEvent` (first layout / rotation) and on a per-tick safe-area/size change; sets the root bg to the app navy (0x141C30, mirrors UiKit.Background — hardcoded because FTS.Services can't reference FTS.Views) so the inset border matches behind the cutout. Off-player Screen.safeArea = full screen → zero padding. (4) **Frame rate / battery:** `Services/FrameRateController.cs` (App-scope `IStartable`+`ITickable`+`IDisposable`): on mobile sets `QualitySettings.vSyncCount = 0`; targets 60fps while interacting OR on the MatchWatch screen (tracked via `ScreenChangedMessage.ScreenName == "MatchWatchScreenPresenter"`), drops to 30fps after 8s of no input (touch/click/key/mouse-move resets the idle timer); applies only on change. Both registered in `AppLifetimeScope` via `RegisterEntryPoint` (live in FTS.Services, no Views dep). (5) **Touch targets + responsive Hub:** `UiKit` += `MinTouchPx = 48` token + `EnsureTapTarget(VisualElement)` helper (MenuButton/PrimaryButton are 54, already compliant); `HubView` nav buttons moved into a wrapping flex row (`flexWrap: Wrap`, `justifyContent: Center`, width 100% / maxWidth 960) → multi-column grid on wide landscape/PC, single thumb-column in portrait, NO orientation branching. Sim path LIVE: `LocalClock` SeasonProgressor and `MatchWatchScreenPresenter` engine both `applyPositioning:true` — the user's shape tilts his side, AI clubs (clean presets, null positions) stay byte-identical, and the watch re-sim reproduces the committed result because the kickoff plan carries the user lineup's positions through `LineupPlan.Materialize`. Loc `squad.pitch_hint` reworded (en+it) for swap-vs-move. All edits (no new client .cs → no new .meta). Play-mode VERIFIED & ACCEPTED by the user (fullback dragged high plays as a Winger; watched result matches the committed one; formation change / Auto Pick reset to the clean preset; AI matches unaffected). **6.10 fully closed [x]. Next roadmap step: 6.11 (pre-match opponent report) — NOT started.** Decisions locked with the user up front (2026-07-02): (1) effect = role-by-zone + a LIGHT continuous tilt (not full continuous, not role-only); (2) Sim.Core + harness FIRST then client (fase-3/5 pattern; balance-sensitive → opt-in default-off so the golden master stays byte-identical); (3) custom positions RESET to the clean preset on a formation change. ALL pure/deterministic, integer math, NO RNG. New files (all Sim.Core): `Match/SlotPosition.cs` (readonly struct, normalized permille — X = line height own-goal 0→attacked-goal 1000, Y = width 0..1000; distinct from the dm `PitchPoint`; clamped, serializable-via-fields but the SAVED form is on the plan, see below); `Tactics/FormationGeometry.cs` (THE single source of the anchor geometry the client mirrors — `AnchorX(role)` from `MatchBalance.FormationAnchorXPermilleByRole`, `AnchorY(roles,i)` spreading same-role peers across the width over `Pitch.WidthDm` exactly like the client `Presenters/FormationLayout` and the 1.5 position-stream BuildAnchors → the client's FormationLayout should DELEGATE to this in 6.10b, killing the duplication); `Tactics/ZoneRole.cs` (`Resolve(baseRole, SlotPosition, PositioningBalance)` → GK always GK; central X-bands CB→DM→CM→AM→ST, and wide `|Y-500| > WideZoneThreshold` promotes the deep band→FullBack / the high bands→Winger [a wide mid-band player = CentralMidfielder, matching the Formations wide-mid encoding]; the band boundaries are chosen so EVERY formation preset anchor maps back to its own role — a clean preset is a FIXED POINT of the map, which is exactly why "reset on formation change" restores the preset roles); `Tactics/PositionalTilt.cs` (`Compute(Lineup, MatchBalance, PositioningBalance)` → `TacticModifiers.Multipliers`: per non-GK slot that carries a custom position, dHeight = avg(actualX − role-anchorX) and dSpread = avg(|actualY−500| − |anchorY−500|), turned into att/mid/def % via the per-100-permille coeffs and clamped to `MaxTiltPercent` 8; a slot sitting on its anchor or with NO position contributes 0 → a clean preset AND a null-position lineup are the identity). Config `PositioningBalance` in BalanceConfig (zone bands DeepBandMaxX 260 / DmBandMaxX 390 / CmBandMaxX 490 / AmBandMaxX 630 / WideForwardMinX 490 / WideZoneThreshold 300; tilt coeffs HeightAttack 2 / HeightDefense 2 / SpreadAttack 1 / SpreadMidfield 1 per-100‰, MaxTiltPercent 8). Domain (additive, NO save-version bump): `LineupSlot.Position` (runtime `SlotPosition?`) + `LineupPlanSlot.PosXPermille`/`PosYPermille` (serializable nullable ints — the client resolves the role via ZoneRole at DROP time and stores the already-resolved `Role`, so `LineupPlan.Materialize` just carries the position through; From/Materialize pass-through, both-null = byte-identical to the pre-6.10 plan). Engine `MatchEngine(…, bool applyPositioning=false)` — when on, ComputeRatings folds each side's PositionalTilt via `WithMultipliers` AFTER the tactics multipliers and BEFORE within-match fatigue; consumes NO RNG and `x*1.0==x`, so flag-off / null / clean-preset is byte-identical (no Version bump, golden masters/replays safe). Threaded through `SeasonProgressor(…, bool applyPositioning=false)` — AI clubs field clean best-XI presets (null positions) so their matches stay byte-identical even with the flag globally on; only the USER's custom shape is affected. NOTE: the 1.5 position STREAM still uses the role anchors, so a custom shape isn't rendered in-match yet — presentation-only follow-up (the effect on the RESULT is fully modelled). Tests `Sim.Core.Tests/Positioning/PositioningTests.cs` (6): `PresetAnchors_RoundTripToTheirRoles` (all 6 formations, every anchor→its own role = the reset-on-formation-change guarantee), `FullBack_DraggedHighAndWide_BecomesWinger` (+ high-central→ST, deep-wide→FB, GK stays GK), `ZoneRole_IsMonotonicInLineHeight_Central` (CB→DM→CM→AM→ST in order), `FlagOff_IgnoresCustomPositions_ByteIdentical` + `CleanPreset_And_NullPositions_AreIdentity_WhenFlagOn` (golden-master safety, JSON-report identity), `SameLineup_SamePositions_IsDeterministic`, **THE ✅** `PushingTheLineUp_ScoresAndConcedesMore` (equal squads, A pushes +200‰: prints `[positioning-line]` GF/GA base→pushed, asserts BOTH rise), `WiderSetup_CreatesMoreThanNarrow` (prints `[positioning-width]`), **anti-exploit ✅** `ParkEveryoneForward_IsNotADominantExploit` (shove everyone forward to saturate the +8/−8 cap, 1000 matches, prints `[positioning-exploit]`, asserts win rate < 0.58 — attack gains paid for defensively → no free lunch). DESIGN NOTES for review: (a) magnitudes are first-pass — the ✅s assert comparative inequalities so they hold regardless of exact calibration; eyeball `[positioning-line]`/`[positioning-width]`/`[positioning-exploit]` and tell me if the tilt feels right (knobs: HeightAttack/HeightDefensePercentPer100, SpreadAttack/SpreadMidfield, MaxTiltPercent); (b) baseline = per-slot RESOLVED-role anchor, so the tilt measures how far a player is from the canonical spot of the role he's now playing — formation-agnostic, and a role change (FB→W) is a discrete jump the tilt then measures from the W anchor; (c) intended client usage = store positions for ALL outfield slots (or none) so the average is over 10, not one, keeping one nudged player a small tilt; (d) subs/re-sim keep whatever positions the active lineup carries. HANDOFF: shared/ changed → run `.\tools\build-simcore.ps1`, then `dotnet test shared/Sim.Core.Tests/Sim.Core.Tests.csproj --logger "console;verbosity=detailed"` and paste `[positioning-line]`, `[positioning-width]`, `[positioning-exploit]` + confirm 205 green & golden master 0xCDEA…unchanged so we judge the tilt together; new .cs files (SlotPosition, FormationGeometry, ZoneRole, PositionalTilt, PositioningTests) need Unity .meta on first import. Keep 6.10 at [~] until the harness is judged; then 6.10b = client (the 6.7 pitch lets a token drop on empty turf → reposition + ZoneRole recompute + reset-to-FormationGeometry-preset on formation change; LineupPlan carries the per-slot positions; LocalClock/MatchWatch engines pass applyPositioning:true — AI matches stay byte-identical).
- **6.11 DONE [x] (pre-match opponent report — user verified in Play-mode, incl. the two-column layout revision, and ACCEPTED; client-only, NO Sim.Core change → 208 tests + golden master 0xCDEA5A2F7B9E5CF6 unchanged, no build-simcore, no save bump).** The 6.9 playtest removed the little "prossimo avversario" preview from the user's own Tactics screen; 6.11 gives a dedicated, opt-in intel screen instead. Decisions with the user up front (2026-07-08): (a) launched from the **Hub next-match card** (a "Studia avversario" button on the status card, shown only while a match is upcoming); (b) **XI/shape/coarse strength ALWAYS visible, per-player attribute detail gated by the 5.4 scouting layer** (scouted overall RANGES, "?" token badge for the unscouted); (c) **PURE read-only** — no counter-tactic shortcut (the pre-match reaches both sides at once → no unfair edge). DATA REALITY that made it fully client-only with NO save bump: AI clubs field a default 4-3-3 with a neutral tactic (they don't choose modules/tactics), so "likely formation" is honestly their fielded 4-3-3 and "tactic" reads "Balanced (AI default)" — no per-club last-setup memory needed. New `Views/OpponentReportView.cs` (dumb, grep-clean of Sim.Core: opponent crest + header, an intel `UiKit.Card` [coarse strength / likely formation / tactic / recent-form colour pills — win green / draw amber / loss red, with the scoreline from the opponent's POV], a mirrored hero `PitchFormationView` [reused from 6.7] for the likely-XI shape, a scouted-squad `ScrollView` of `PlayerRowKit` rows [name · reparto-coloured role · age · scouted OVR], a Back footer, and a "no upcoming match" empty state when the season's complete). New `Presenters/OpponentReportScreenPresenter.cs` (Fts.Presenters; deps ScreenNavigator/CareerState/ScoutingService/ClubIdentityService/PlayerProfileTarget/ILocalizationService — all Game-scope, DI-resolved via `Push<T>`, no manual call site): finds the user's earliest unplayed `Season.Fixtures` → opponent + venue; opponent crest from `ClubIdentityService.Visual`; coarse strength = best-XI (`LineupSelector.BestEleven`) average `PlayerRating.OverallFor`; shape tokens via `FormationLayout.Normalized` in the opponent's kit colours with a scouted-OVR-or-"?" badge; recent form = the opponent's last 5 played fixtures from their perspective; the squad list uses `ScoutingService.Report` (overall range narrowing with knowledge, single number when fully scouted), and tapping a row opens the 4.6 Player profile via `PlayerProfileTarget` (which already shows non-owned players as scouted ranges + hidden condition, 5.4b). Launch wiring: `HubView` gained an `OpponentReportClicked` button on the status card + `SetOpponentReportVisible`; `HubPresenter` subscribes, pushes `OpponentReportScreenPresenter`, toggles the button on whether a next fixture exists (hidden when the season's complete). Nothing writes back → the opponent never leaks into the user's own Tactics screen. Loc `hub.opponent_report` + `opponent.*` (14 keys/side: title/home/away/strength/formation/tactic/tactic_neutral/form_caption/no_form/shape_caption/roster_caption/none) en+it at parity (placeholder counts matched; reuses `scouting.ovr_range`/`ovr_known`, `role.*`, `common.back`; validated via the Read tool — the bash mount lagged the file-API writes AGAIN, tripping json.load on a stale partial read while the file API showed both files well-formed and at parity). New client `.cs` (OpponentReportView, OpponentReportScreenPresenter) need Unity `.meta` on first import; HubView/HubPresenter are edits. Play-mode check: on the Hub with a match upcoming, tap "Studia avversario" → the report opens (crest, coarse strength, 4-3-3 shape on a mirrored pitch, recent-form pills, squad with scouted OVR ranges); tap a squad player → his scouted profile; a fully-played season hides the button and shows the empty state; toggle language → all labels translate. **LAYOUT REVISION after the first Play-mode screenshot (user: "non centrale — campo più grande a sinistra, lista a destra senza scrollbar"): OpponentReportView reworked from the narrow 720px centred column into a full-width two-column body — compact crest+title top bar + full-width intel card (`flexShrink:0` so the form pills no longer clip), then a body row with a LARGE pitch on the LEFT (flexGrow 1.7, fills height) and the scouted squad list on the RIGHT (flexGrow 1) in a ScrollView with BOTH scrollbars hidden (wheel/drag still scroll); below 760px the two columns stack (pitch 320px over the list) via a GeometryChanged handler. Presenter/loc/view-API unchanged — still Play-mode untested.**
- Match engine calibration (accepted by user): GoalCoefficient 0.92 → avg 2.44 goals/match, draws ~25%, team scoring 3+ in ~24% of matches, strong-vs-weak 82%, home advantage +9pp on home wins.
- Known quirks: generated defense ratings sit slightly above attack ratings (role-weight inflation) — GoalCoefficient compensates; extra per-match variance will come from form/fitness in 4.1.
